- [ ] **Step 2: Ejecutar las pruebas y verlas fallar**

Run: `pnpm test:db tests/db/menu-functions.test.ts`
Expected: FAIL con `function public.save_dish(...) does not exist`, `function public.get_menu_tree(unknown) does not exist`…

- [ ] **Step 3: Escribir la migración**

Create `supabase/migrations/20261126000200_menu_funciones.sql`:

```sql
-- Mesa · Hito 3 · funciones del panel del menú: leer todo el menú, guardar platos con sus precios,
-- duplicar, ordenar y agotados. Las de edición son SECURITY INVOKER: se aplican las RLS del usuario.

/** Todo el menú del restaurante para el panel (también las cartas desactivadas), en una sola llamada. */
create or replace function public.get_menu_tree(p_restaurant_id uuid) returns jsonb
language plpgsql stable security invoker set search_path = '' as $$
declare
  r public.restaurants%rowtype;
begin
  if not (public.is_member(p_restaurant_id) or public.is_platform_admin()) then
    raise exception 'FORBIDDEN' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  select * into r from public.restaurants x where x.id = p_restaurant_id;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'FORBIDDEN' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  return jsonb_build_object(
    'enabled_locales', to_jsonb(r.enabled_locales),
    'unavailable_mode', r.menu_unavailable_mode,
    'timezone', r.timezone,
    'menus', coalesce((
      select jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
               'id', m.id, 'kind', m.kind, 'names', m.names, 'includes', m.includes, 'active', m.active,
               'position', m.position, 'price_cents', m.price_cents, 'weekdays', to_jsonb(m.weekdays),
               'start_minute', m.start_minute, 'end_minute', m.end_minute, 'date_from', m.date_from, 'date_to', m.date_to,
               'sections', coalesce((
                 select jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
                          'id', s.id, 'names', s.names, 'position', s.position,
                          'items', coalesce((
                            select jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
                                     'id', i.id, 'position', i.position, 'dish_id', i.dish_id,
                                     'inline_names', i.inline_names, 'inline_allergens', to_jsonb(i.inline_allergens)
                                   ) order by i.position, i.created_at)
                              from public.menu_items i where i.section_id = s.id
                          ), '[]'::jsonb)
                        ) order by s.position, s.created_at)
                   from public.menu_sections s where s.menu_id = m.id
               ), '[]'::jsonb)
             ) order by m.position, m.created_at)
        from public.menus m where m.restaurant_id = p_restaurant_id
    ), '[]'::jsonb),
    'dishes', coalesce((
      select jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
               'id', d.id, 'names', d.names, 'descriptions', d.descriptions, 'photo_path', d.photo_path,
               'allergens', to_jsonb(d.allergens), 'tags', to_jsonb(d.tags), 'unavailable', d.unavailable,
               'prices', coalesce((
                 select jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('kind', p.kind, 'price_cents', p.price_cents, 'labels', p.labels)
                          order by p.position, p.created_at)
                   from public.dish_prices p where p.dish_id = d.id
               ), '[]'::jsonb)
             ) order by d.created_at, d.id)
        from public.dishes d where d.restaurant_id = p_restaurant_id
    ), '[]'::jsonb)
  );
end $$;

/**
 * Crea (p_dish_id null) o actualiza un plato y sustituye sus precios, todo o nada.
 * p_prices: [{"kind": "fixed", "price_cents": 1250, "labels": {"es": "Ración"}}, …] en orden; como mucho 4.
 */
create or replace function public.save_dish(
  p_restaurant_id uuid, p_dish_id uuid, p_names jsonb, p_descriptions jsonb,
  p_allergens text[], p_tags text[], p_prices jsonb
) returns uuid language plpgsql security invoker set search_path = '' as $$
declare
  v_id uuid := p_dish_id;
  v_price jsonb;
  v_position integer := 0;
begin
  if not public.has_role(p_restaurant_id, '{admin,manager}') then
    raise exception 'FORBIDDEN' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if jsonb_typeof(p_prices) is distinct from 'array' or jsonb_array_length(p_prices) > 4 then
    raise exception 'INVALID_INPUT' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if v_id is null then
    insert into public.dishes (restaurant_id, names, descriptions, allergens, tags)
    values (p_restaurant_id, p_names, coalesce(p_descriptions, '{}'::jsonb), coalesce(p_allergens, '{}'), coalesce(p_tags, '{}'))
    returning id into v_id;
  else
    update public.dishes
       set names = p_names, descriptions = coalesce(p_descriptions, '{}'::jsonb),
           allergens = coalesce(p_allergens, '{}'), tags = coalesce(p_tags, '{}')
     where id = v_id and restaurant_id = p_restaurant_id;
    if not found then
      raise exception 'FORBIDDEN' using errcode = '42501';
    end if;
    delete from public.dish_prices p where p.dish_id = v_id;
  end if;
  for v_price in select e.value from jsonb_array_elements(p_prices) e loop
    insert into public.dish_prices (restaurant_id, dish_id, kind, price_cents, labels, position)
    values (p_restaurant_id, v_id, (v_price ->> 'kind')::public.price_kind, (v_price ->> 'price_cents')::integer,
            coalesce(v_price -> 'labels', '{}'::jsonb), v_position);
    v_position := v_position + 1;
  end loop;
  return v_id;
end $$;

/** Copia un plato (sin foto ni agotado). Con p_section_id, coloca la copia al final de esa sección. */
create or replace function public.duplicate_dish(p_dish_id uuid, p_section_id uuid) returns uuid
language plpgsql security invoker set search_path = '' as $$
declare
  d public.dishes%rowtype;
  v_id uuid;
begin
  select * into d from public.dishes x where x.id = p_dish_id;
  if not found or not public.has_role(d.restaurant_id, '{admin,manager}') then
    raise exception 'FORBIDDEN' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  insert into public.dishes (restaurant_id, names, descriptions, allergens, tags)
  values (d.restaurant_id, jsonb_set(d.names, '{es}', to_jsonb(left(d.names ->> 'es', 112) || ' (copia)')),
          d.descriptions, d.allergens, d.tags)
  returning id into v_id;
  insert into public.dish_prices (restaurant_id, dish_id, kind, price_cents, labels, position)
  select p.restaurant_id, v_id, p.kind, p.price_cents, p.labels, p.position
    from public.dish_prices p where p.dish_id = p_dish_id order by p.position;
  if p_section_id is not null then
    insert into public.menu_items (restaurant_id, section_id, dish_id, position)
    values (d.restaurant_id, p_section_id, v_id,
            coalesce((select max(i.position) + 1 from public.menu_items i where i.section_id = p_section_id), 0));
  end if;
  return v_id;
end $$;

/** Posiciones 0, 1, 2… de las cartas en el orden de p_ids, que trae exactamente todas las del restaurante. */
create or replace function public.reorder_menus(p_restaurant_id uuid, p_ids uuid[]) returns void
language plpgsql security invoker set search_path = '' as $$
begin
  if not public.has_role(p_restaurant_id, '{admin,manager}') then
    raise exception 'FORBIDDEN' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if p_ids is null or array(select x from unnest(p_ids) x order by x)
       is distinct from array(select m.id from public.menus m where m.restaurant_id = p_restaurant_id order by m.id) then
    raise exception 'INVALID_INPUT' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  update public.menus m set position = o.ord - 1
    from unnest(p_ids) with ordinality as o(id, ord)
   where m.id = o.id and m.position is distinct from o.ord - 1;
end $$;

create or replace function public.reorder_sections(p_menu_id uuid, p_ids uuid[]) returns void
language plpgsql security invoker set search_path = '' as $$
declare
  v_restaurant uuid;
begin
  select m.restaurant_id into v_restaurant from public.menus m where m.id = p_menu_id;
  if v_restaurant is null or not public.has_role(v_restaurant, '{admin,manager}') then
    raise exception 'FORBIDDEN' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if p_ids is null or array(select x from unnest(p_ids) x order by x)
       is distinct from array(select s.id from public.menu_sections s where s.menu_id = p_menu_id order by s.id) then
    raise exception 'INVALID_INPUT' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  update public.menu_sections s set position = o.ord - 1
    from unnest(p_ids) with ordinality as o(id, ord)
   where s.id = o.id and s.position is distinct from o.ord - 1;
end $$;

create or replace function public.reorder_items(p_section_id uuid, p_ids uuid[]) returns void
language plpgsql security invoker set search_path = '' as $$
declare
  v_restaurant uuid;
begin
  select s.restaurant_id into v_restaurant from public.menu_sections s where s.id = p_section_id;
  if v_restaurant is null or not public.has_role(v_restaurant, '{admin,manager}') then
    raise exception 'FORBIDDEN' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if p_ids is null or array(select x from unnest(p_ids) x order by x)
       is distinct from array(select i.id from public.menu_items i where i.section_id = p_section_id order by i.id) then
    raise exception 'INVALID_INPUT' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  update public.menu_items i set position = o.ord - 1
    from unnest(p_ids) with ordinality as o(id, ord)
   where i.id = o.id and i.position is distinct from o.ord - 1;
end $$;

/** Marcar o quitar «Agotado». También Sala, que no tiene UPDATE efectivo sobre dishes. */
create or replace function public.set_dish_unavailable(p_dish_id uuid, p_unavailable boolean) returns void
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare
  v_restaurant uuid;
begin
  select d.restaurant_id into v_restaurant from public.dishes d where d.id = p_dish_id;
  if v_restaurant is null or not public.has_role(v_restaurant, '{admin,manager,staff}') then
    raise exception 'FORBIDDEN' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if p_unavailable is null then
    raise exception 'INVALID_INPUT' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  update public.dishes set unavailable = p_unavailable where id = p_dish_id;
end $$;

/** Cómo salen los agotados en la carta pública: ocultos (hide) o tachados (strike). */
create or replace function public.set_menu_unavailable_mode(p_restaurant_id uuid, p_mode public.menu_unavailable_mode)
returns void language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
begin
  if not public.has_role(p_restaurant_id, '{admin,manager}') then
    raise exception 'FORBIDDEN' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if p_mode is null then
    raise exception 'INVALID_INPUT' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  update public.restaurants set menu_unavailable_mode = p_mode where id = p_restaurant_id;
end $$;

revoke execute on function public.get_menu_tree(uuid),
  public.save_dish(uuid, uuid, jsonb, jsonb, text[], text[], jsonb), public.duplicate_dish(uuid, uuid),
  public.reorder_menus(uuid, uuid[]), public.reorder_sections(uuid, uuid[]), public.reorder_items(uuid, uuid[]),
  public.set_dish_unavailable(uuid, boolean), public.set_menu_unavailable_mode(uuid, public.menu_unavailable_mode)
  from public, anon;
grant execute on function public.get_menu_tree(uuid),
  public.save_dish(uuid, uuid, jsonb, jsonb, text[], text[], jsonb), public.duplicate_dish(uuid, uuid),
  public.reorder_menus(uuid, uuid[]), public.reorder_sections(uuid, uuid[]), public.reorder_items(uuid, uuid[]),
  public.set_dish_unavailable(uuid, boolean), public.set_menu_unavailable_mode(uuid, public.menu_unavailable_mode)
  to authenticated;
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Escribir la migración**

Create `supabase/migrations/20261126000300_menu_fotos.sql`:

```sql
-- Mesa · Hito 3 · fotos de platos: la carpeta {restaurante}/dishes/ la gestionan Administrador y Encargado.
-- El resto del bucket (logo, portada y galería) sigue siendo solo del Administrador, como en 20261125000100_local.sql.
-- Las políticas restaurant_media_* ya llaman a esta función: basta con sustituirla.
create or replace function public.can_manage_media(p_bucket_id text, p_name text) returns boolean
language plpgsql stable set search_path = '' as $$
declare
  v_folder constant text := '^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}/';
begin
  if p_bucket_id is distinct from 'restaurant-media' or p_name is null then
    return false;
  end if;
  if p_name ~ (v_folder || 'dishes/[a-z0-9-]+\.(webp|jpg)$') then
    return public.has_role(((storage.foldername(p_name))[1])::uuid, '{admin,manager}');
  end if;
  if p_name ~ (v_folder || '(logo-[a-z0-9-]+|cover-[a-z0-9-]+|gallery/[a-z0-9-]+)\.(webp|jpg)$') then
    return public.has_role(((storage.foldername(p_name))[1])::uuid, '{admin}');
  end if;
  return false;
end $$;

-- create or replace conserva los permisos; se repiten para que la migración se entienda sola.
revoke execute on function public.can_manage_media(text, text) from public, anon;
grant execute on function public.can_manage_media(text, text) to authenticated;
```

### Task 4: Migración de la carta pública para anon

**Files:**
- Create: `supabase/migrations/20261126000400_menu_publico.sql`
- Test: `tests/db/menu-public.test.ts`
- Modify: `tests/db/public.test.ts` (la prueba de las funciones que ejecuta anon)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 1–2 y `makePublishable` (para activar restaurantes sin tropezar con `check_published_fields`).
- Produces:
  - `public.i18n_pick(p_value jsonb, p_locales text[]) returns jsonb` — interna (sin `execute` para nadie): deja solo esos idiomas.
  - `public.get_public_menu(p_slug text) returns jsonb` — SECURITY DEFINER, `anon` y `authenticated`. `null` si el slug no es de un restaurante `active` (los slugs antiguos los resuelve `get_public_restaurant`). Forma: `{"unavailable_mode": "strike", "menus": [{"id", "kind", "names", "price_cents", "includes", "weekdays", "start_minute", "end_minute", "date_from", "date_to", "sections": [{"id", "names", "items": [{"id", "inline_names", "inline_allergens", "dish": null | {"id", "names", "descriptions", "photo_path", "allergens", "tags", "unavailable", "prices": [{"kind", "price_cents", "labels"}]}}]}]}]}`. Solo cartas **activas**, por `position`; textos filtrados a `enabled_locales`.
  - `public.list_public_menus() returns table (slug text, enabled_locales text[], updated_at timestamptz)` — SECURITY DEFINER, `anon` y `authenticated`: restaurantes activos con al menos una carta activa con algún elemento; `updated_at` = lo más reciente de restaurante, cartas activas y platos.

Lee antes: `get_public_restaurant` y `list_public_slugs` en `supabase/migrations/20261125000300_publicar.sql`, y el final de `tests/db/public.test.ts`.

Create `tests/db/menu-public.test.ts`:

```ts
import { afterAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type pg from 'pg';
import {
  addMember,
  as,
  asSuperuser,
  createUser,
  insertDish,
  insertItem,
  insertMenu,
  insertRestaurant,
  insertSection,
  makePublishable,
  pool,
  tx,
} from './helpers';

afterAll(() => pool.end());

/** Restaurante publicado (status active) con castellano e inglés activos. */
async function activeRestaurant(c: pg.PoolClient, slug = 'la-taberna'): Promise<string> {
  const id = await insertRestaurant(c, { name: 'La Taberna', slug, status: 'setup' });
  await makePublishable(c, id);
  await c.query("update public.restaurants set status = 'active', enabled_locales = '{es,en}' where id = $1", [id]);
  return id;
}

async function publicMenu(c: pg.PoolClient, slug: string) {
  await as(c, null);
  const { rows } = await c.query('select public.get_public_menu($1) as menu', [slug]);
  return rows[0].menu;
}

type Named = { names: { es: string } };

describe('get_public_menu', () => {
  it('anon lee las cartas activas con secciones, platos y precios, en orden y solo en los idiomas activos', () =>
    tx(async (c) => {
      const r = await activeRestaurant(c);
      const carta = await insertMenu(c, r, { name: 'Carta', position: 1 });
      await insertMenu(c, r, { name: 'Bebidas', position: 0 });
      const entrantes = await insertSection(c, r, carta, 'Entrantes', 0);
      const postres = await insertSection(c, r, carta, 'Postres', 1);
      const croquetas = await insertDish(c, r, {
        names: { es: 'Croquetas', en: 'Croquettes', ca: 'Croquetes' },
        allergens: ['gluten', 'milk'],
        prices: [{ cents: 900, labels: { es: 'Media', en: 'Half', fr: 'Demi' } }, { cents: 1500, labels: { es: 'Ración' } }],
      });
      const flan = await insertDish(c, r, { name: 'Flan', allergens: ['eggs', 'milk'], prices: [{ cents: 500 }] });
      await insertItem(c, r, postres, { dishId: flan });
      await insertItem(c, r, entrantes, { dishId: croquetas });
      const menu = await publicMenu(c, 'La-Taberna ');
      expect(menu.unavailable_mode).toBe('strike');
      expect(menu.menus.map((m: Named) => m.names.es)).toEqual(['Bebidas', 'Carta']);
      expect(menu.menus[0].sections).toEqual([]);
      const sections = menu.menus[1].sections;
      expect(sections.map((s: Named) => s.names.es)).toEqual(['Entrantes', 'Postres']);
      const dish = sections[0].items[0].dish;
      expect(dish.names).toEqual({ es: 'Croquetas', en: 'Croquettes' });
      expect(dish.allergens).toEqual(['gluten', 'milk']);
      expect(dish.prices).toEqual([
        { kind: 'fixed', price_cents: 900, labels: { es: 'Media', en: 'Half' } },
        { kind: 'fixed', price_cents: 1500, labels: { es: 'Ración' } },
      ]);
      expect(sections[1].items[0].dish.names).toEqual({ es: 'Flan' });
    }));

  it('nunca devuelve cartas desactivadas', () =>
    tx(async (c) => {
      const r = await activeRestaurant(c);
      const off = await insertMenu(c, r, { name: 'Antigua', active: false });
      await insertSection(c, r, off);
      await insertMenu(c, r, { name: 'Carta', position: 1 });
      const menu = await publicMenu(c, 'la-taberna');
      expect(menu.menus.map((m: Named) => m.names.es)).toEqual(['Carta']);
    }));

  it('restaurantes en configuración o desactivados, slugs antiguos y slugs que no existen → null', () =>
    tx(async (c) => {
      const setup = await insertRestaurant(c, { name: 'En obras', slug: 'en-obras', status: 'setup' });
      const off = await insertRestaurant(c, { name: 'Cerrado', slug: 'cerrado', status: 'disabled' });
      for (const id of [setup, off]) await insertMenu(c, id);
      const r = await activeRestaurant(c);
      await insertMenu(c, r);
      await asSuperuser(c);
      await c.query("insert into public.slug_redirects (old_slug, restaurant_id) values ('vieja-taberna', $1)", [r]);
      for (const slug of ['en-obras', 'cerrado', 'vieja-taberna', 'no-existe', '']) {
        expect(await publicMenu(c, slug), slug).toBeNull();
      }
    }));

  it('el menú del día trae precio, «incluye» y programación; los platos escritos a mano, sus alérgenos', () =>
    tx(async (c) => {
      const r = await activeRestaurant(c);
      const daily = await insertMenu(c, r, { kind: 'daily', priceCents: 1450, weekdays: [1, 2, 3, 4, 5], startMinute: 780, endMinute: 960 });
      await asSuperuser(c);
      await c.query('update public.menus set includes = $2 where id = $1', [daily, JSON.stringify({ es: 'Pan, bebida y postre', de: 'Brot' })]);
      const primeros = await insertSection(c, r, daily, 'Primeros');
      await insertItem(c, r, primeros, { inlineName: 'Sopa de pescado', inlineAllergens: ['fish', 'celery'] });
      const menu = await publicMenu(c, 'la-taberna');
      const d = menu.menus[0];
      expect(d).toMatchObject({
        kind: 'daily',
        price_cents: 1450,
        includes: { es: 'Pan, bebida y postre' },
        weekdays: [1, 2, 3, 4, 5],
        start_minute: 780,
        end_minute: 960,
        date_from: null,
        date_to: null,
      });
      expect(d.sections[0].items[0]).toMatchObject({ dish: null, inline_names: { es: 'Sopa de pescado' }, inline_allergens: ['fish', 'celery'] });
      const special = await insertMenu(c, r, { kind: 'special', name: 'Nochebuena', position: 1, dateFrom: '2026-12-24', dateTo: '2026-12-24' });
      expect(special).toBeTruthy();
      const again = await publicMenu(c, 'la-taberna');
      expect(again.menus[1]).toMatchObject({ kind: 'special', date_from: '2026-12-24', date_to: '2026-12-24' });
    }));

  it('solo devuelve las claves públicas', () =>
    tx(async (c) => {
      const r = await activeRestaurant(c);
      const carta = await insertMenu(c, r);
      const section = await insertSection(c, r, carta);
      await insertItem(c, r, section, { dishId: await insertDish(c, r, { prices: [{ cents: 900 }] }) });
      const menu = await publicMenu(c, 'la-taberna');
      expect(Object.keys(menu).sort()).toEqual(['menus', 'unavailable_mode']);
      expect(Object.keys(menu.menus[0]).sort()).toEqual(
        ['date_from', 'date_to', 'end_minute', 'id', 'includes', 'kind', 'names', 'price_cents', 'sections', 'start_minute', 'weekdays'],
      );
      expect(Object.keys(menu.menus[0].sections[0]).sort()).toEqual(['id', 'items', 'names']);
      expect(Object.keys(menu.menus[0].sections[0].items[0]).sort()).toEqual(['dish', 'id', 'inline_allergens', 'inline_names']);
      expect(Object.keys(menu.menus[0].sections[0].items[0].dish).sort()).toEqual(
        ['allergens', 'descriptions', 'id', 'names', 'photo_path', 'prices', 'tags', 'unavailable'],
      );
    }));

  it('el modo de agotados y los agotados viajan con los datos', () =>
    tx(async (c) => {
      const r = await activeRestaurant(c);
      const admin = await createUser(c, 'admin@example.com');
      await addMember(c, r, admin.id, 'admin');
      const carta = await insertMenu(c, r);
      const section = await insertSection(c, r, carta);
      await insertItem(c, r, section, { dishId: await insertDish(c, r, { unavailable: true }) });
      await as(c, admin);
      await c.query("select public.set_menu_unavailable_mode($1, 'hide')", [r]);
      const menu = await publicMenu(c, 'la-taberna');
      expect(menu.unavailable_mode).toBe('hide');
      expect(menu.menus[0].sections[0].items[0].dish.unavailable).toBe(true);
    }));
});

describe('list_public_menus', () => {
  it('solo restaurantes activos con alguna carta activa con platos', () =>
    tx(async (c) => {
      const conCarta = await activeRestaurant(c, 'con-carta');
      const carta = await insertMenu(c, conCarta);
      await insertItem(c, conCarta, await insertSection(c, conCarta, carta), { dishId: await insertDish(c, conCarta) });
      const vacia = await activeRestaurant(c, 'carta-vacia');
      await insertSection(c, vacia, await insertMenu(c, vacia));
      const apagada = await activeRestaurant(c, 'carta-apagada');
      const off = await insertMenu(c, apagada, { active: false });
      await insertItem(c, apagada, await insertSection(c, apagada, off), { dishId: await insertDish(c, apagada) });
      const enObras = await insertRestaurant(c, { name: 'En obras', slug: 'en-obras', status: 'setup' });
      const obras = await insertMenu(c, enObras);
      await insertItem(c, enObras, await insertSection(c, enObras, obras), { dishId: await insertDish(c, enObras) });
      await as(c, null);
      const { rows } = await c.query('select slug, enabled_locales, updated_at from public.list_public_menus()');
      expect(rows.map((x) => [x.slug, x.enabled_locales])).toEqual([['con-carta', ['es', 'en']]]);
      expect(rows[0].updated_at).toBeInstanceOf(Date);
    }));
});

describe('privilegios del Hito 3', () => {
  it('nadie ejecuta los triggers nuevos ni i18n_pick; anon tampoco las funciones del panel', () =>
    tx(async (c) => {
      for (const role of ['anon', 'authenticated']) {
        for (const fn of [
          'public.limit_menu_rows()',
          'public.check_dish_prices()',
          'public.check_menu_item()',
          'public.i18n_pick(jsonb, text[])',
        ]) {
          const { rows } = await c.query('select has_function_privilege($1, $2, $3) as ok', [role, fn, 'execute']);
          expect(rows[0].ok, `${role} → ${fn}`).toBe(false);
        }
      }
      for (const fn of [
        'public.get_menu_tree(uuid)',
        'public.save_dish(uuid, uuid, jsonb, jsonb, text[], text[], jsonb)',
        'public.set_dish_unavailable(uuid, boolean)',
        'public.valid_i18n(jsonb, integer, boolean)',
      ]) {
        const { rows } = await c.query("select has_function_privilege('anon', $1, 'execute') as ok", [fn]);
        expect(rows[0].ok, fn).toBe(false);
      }
    }));
});
```

Run: `pnpm test:db`
Expected: FAIL en `menu-public.test.ts` (`function public.get_public_menu(unknown) does not exist`, `function public.list_public_menus() does not exist`, `function "public.i18n_pick(jsonb, text[])" does not exist`) y en la prueba modificada de `public.test.ts` (faltan `get_public_menu` y `list_public_menus`).

- [ ] **Step 3: Escribir la migración**

Create `supabase/migrations/20261126000400_menu_publico.sql`:

```sql
-- Mesa · Hito 3 · carta pública para anon: solo cartas activas de restaurantes activos, en sus idiomas activos.
-- Las cartas traen su programación: qué menú del día se ve lo decide la página al pintarse (zona del restaurante).

/** Solo los idiomas pedidos de un texto por idioma. Uso interno de get_public_menu. */
create or replace function public.i18n_pick(p_value jsonb, p_locales text[]) returns jsonb
language sql immutable set search_path = '' as $$
  select coalesce(jsonb_object_agg(e.key, e.value), '{}'::jsonb)
    from jsonb_each(coalesce(p_value, '{}'::jsonb)) e
   where e.key = any (p_locales)
$$;

/**
 * Carta pública de un restaurante activo: {"unavailable_mode": …, "menus": […]}, o null.
 * Nunca devuelve cartas desactivadas ni nada de restaurantes en configuración o desactivados.
 */
create or replace function public.get_public_menu(p_slug text) returns jsonb
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare
  v_slug text := lower(trim(coalesce(p_slug, '')));
  r public.restaurants%rowtype;
begin
  select * into r from public.restaurants x where x.slug = v_slug and x.status = 'active';
  if not found then
    return null;
  end if;
  return jsonb_build_object(
    'unavailable_mode', r.menu_unavailable_mode,
    'menus', coalesce((
      select jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
               'id', m.id, 'kind', m.kind, 'names', public.i18n_pick(m.names, r.enabled_locales),
               'price_cents', m.price_cents, 'includes', public.i18n_pick(m.includes, r.enabled_locales),
               'weekdays', to_jsonb(m.weekdays), 'start_minute', m.start_minute, 'end_minute', m.end_minute,
               'date_from', m.date_from, 'date_to', m.date_to,
               'sections', coalesce((
                 select jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
                          'id', s.id, 'names', public.i18n_pick(s.names, r.enabled_locales),
                          'items', coalesce((
                            select jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
                                     'id', i.id,
                                     'inline_names', case when i.inline_names is null then null
                                                          else public.i18n_pick(i.inline_names, r.enabled_locales) end,
                                     'inline_allergens', to_jsonb(i.inline_allergens),
                                     'dish', case when d.id is null then null else jsonb_build_object(
                                       'id', d.id,
                                       'names', public.i18n_pick(d.names, r.enabled_locales),
                                       'descriptions', public.i18n_pick(d.descriptions, r.enabled_locales),
                                       'photo_path', d.photo_path,
                                       'allergens', to_jsonb(d.allergens),
                                       'tags', to_jsonb(d.tags),
                                       'unavailable', d.unavailable,
                                       'prices', coalesce((
                                         select jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
                                                  'kind', p.kind, 'price_cents', p.price_cents,
                                                  'labels', public.i18n_pick(p.labels, r.enabled_locales)
                                                ) order by p.position, p.created_at)
                                           from public.dish_prices p where p.dish_id = d.id
                                       ), '[]'::jsonb)
                                     ) end
                                   ) order by i.position, i.created_at)
                              from public.menu_items i left join public.dishes d on d.id = i.dish_id
                             where i.section_id = s.id
                          ), '[]'::jsonb)
                        ) order by s.position, s.created_at)
                   from public.menu_sections s where s.menu_id = m.id
               ), '[]'::jsonb)
             ) order by m.position, m.created_at)
        from public.menus m
       where m.restaurant_id = r.id and m.active
    ), '[]'::jsonb)
  );
end $$;

/** Para sitemap.xml: restaurantes activos con alguna carta activa que tenga platos. */
create or replace function public.list_public_menus()
returns table (slug text, enabled_locales text[], updated_at timestamptz)
language sql stable security definer set search_path = '' as $$
  select r.slug, r.enabled_locales,
         greatest(r.updated_at,
                  (select max(m.updated_at) from public.menus m where m.restaurant_id = r.id and m.active),
                  (select max(d.updated_at) from public.dishes d where d.restaurant_id = r.id))
    from public.restaurants r
   where r.status = 'active'
     and exists (
       select 1 from public.menus m join public.menu_items i on i.menu_id = m.id
        where m.restaurant_id = r.id and m.active
     )
   order by r.slug
$$;

revoke execute on function public.i18n_pick(jsonb, text[]) from public, anon, authenticated;
revoke execute on function public.get_public_menu(text), public.list_public_menus() from public, anon;
grant execute on function public.get_public_menu(text), public.list_public_menus() to anon, authenticated;
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  ALLERGEN_LABELS_ES,
  ALLERGENS,
  DISH_TAGS,
  isAllergen,
  isDishTag,
  isMenuKind,
  isPriceKind,
  isUnavailableMode,
  MAX_PRICE_CENTS,
  MENU_LIMITS,
  sortAllergens,
  sortTags,
  TAG_LABELS_ES,
  tagAllergenConflict,
} from './codes';

describe('códigos del menú', () => {
  it('14 alérgenos y 6 etiquetas con nombre en castellano, y los límites de la BD', () => {
    expect(ALLERGENS).toHaveLength(14);
    expect(DISH_TAGS).toHaveLength(6);
    expect(Object.keys(ALLERGEN_LABELS_ES)).toEqual([...ALLERGENS]);
    expect(Object.keys(TAG_LABELS_ES)).toEqual([...DISH_TAGS]);
    expect(MENU_LIMITS).toEqual({ menus: 20, sections: 40, items: 100, dishes: 500, prices: 4 });
    expect(MAX_PRICE_CENTS).toBe(9_999_999);
  });

  it('las comprobaciones de tipo solo aceptan códigos conocidos', () => {
    expect(isAllergen('sulphites')).toBe(true);
    expect(isAllergen('pollen')).toBe(false);
    expect(isDishTag('gluten_free')).toBe(true);
    expect(isDishTag('halal')).toBe(false);
    expect(isMenuKind('daily')).toBe(true);
    expect(isMenuKind('brunch')).toBe(false);
    expect(isPriceKind('per_100g')).toBe(true);
    expect(isPriceKind(12)).toBe(false);
    expect(isUnavailableMode('hide')).toBe(true);
    expect(isUnavailableMode('blur')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('sortAllergens y sortTags quitan lo desconocido y los repetidos, en el orden de siempre', () => {
    expect(sortAllergens(['molluscs', 'gluten', 'pollen', 'gluten', 'milk'])).toEqual(['gluten', 'milk', 'molluscs']);
    expect(sortTags(['recommended', 'vegan', 'halal', 'vegan'])).toEqual(['vegan', 'recommended']);
  });

  it('tagAllergenConflict sigue la misma regla que la BD', () => {
    expect(tagAllergenConflict(['gluten_free'], ['gluten'])).toBe('Un plato con gluten no puede llevar la etiqueta «Sin gluten».');
    expect(tagAllergenConflict(['vegan'], ['soy', 'eggs'])).toBe('Un plato con huevos no puede llevar la etiqueta «Vegano».');
    expect(tagAllergenConflict(['vegan'], ['crustaceans'])).toBe('Un plato con crustáceos no puede llevar la etiqueta «Vegano».');
    expect(tagAllergenConflict(['vegan', 'gluten_free'], ['nuts', 'sesame'])).toBeNull();
    expect(tagAllergenConflict(['vegetarian'], ['milk', 'eggs'])).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { i18nFromForm, localeKeys, missingLocales, textFor, toI18nText } from './i18n-text';

describe('textos por idioma', () => {
  it('textFor usa el idioma pedido y, si falta, el castellano', () => {
    const names = { es: 'Croquetas', en: 'Croquettes' };
    expect(textFor(names, 'en')).toBe('Croquettes');
    expect(textFor(names, 'de')).toBe('Croquetas');
    expect(textFor({ es: 'Croquetas', en: '  ' }, 'en')).toBe('Croquetas');
    expect(textFor({}, 'es')).toBeNull();
    expect(textFor(null, 'ca')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('missingLocales dice qué idiomas activos no tienen traducción', () => {
    expect(missingLocales({ es: 'Croquetas', ca: 'Croquetes' }, ['es', 'en', 'ca'])).toEqual(['en']);
    expect(missingLocales(undefined, ['es'])).toEqual(['es']);
  });

  it('campos de formulario por idioma ↔ texto por idioma', () => {
    expect(localeKeys('name')).toEqual(['name_es', 'name_en', 'name_ca', 'name_fr', 'name_de', 'name_it']);
    expect(i18nFromForm({ name_es: ' Croquetas ', name_en: '', name_xx: 'Nope', name_de: 'Kroketten' }, 'name')).toEqual({
      es: 'Croquetas',
      de: 'Kroketten',
    });
    expect(toI18nText({ es: 'Pan', en: 3, xx: 'No', fr: ' ' })).toEqual({ es: 'Pan' });
    expect(toI18nText(null)).toEqual({});
    expect(toI18nText(['es'])).toEqual({});
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { centsToInput, formatCents, formatDishPrice, parsePriceInput } from './price';

/** Los espacios del formato son duros (U+00A0): no se parten al final de línea. */
const nb = (s: string) => s.replace(/ /g, ' ');
const ES = { perKg: '/kg', per100g: '/100 g', market: 'S/M' };
const EN = { perKg: '/kg', per100g: '/100 g', market: 'Market price' };

describe('precios', () => {
  it('formatCents con el formato de cada idioma', () => {
    expect(formatCents(1250)).toBe(nb('12,50 €'));
    expect(formatCents(0, 'es')).toBe(nb('0,00 €'));
    expect(formatCents(123450, 'es')).toBe(nb('1234,50 €'));
    expect(formatCents(1234550, 'es')).toBe(nb('12.345,50 €'));
    for (const locale of ['ca', 'fr', 'de', 'it'] as const) expect(formatCents(1250, locale), locale).toBe(nb('12,50 €'));
    expect(formatCents(1250, 'en')).toBe('€12.50');
  });

  it('formatDishPrice: fijo, por kilo, por 100 g y según mercado', () => {
    expect(formatDishPrice({ kind: 'fixed', priceCents: 1250 }, 'es', ES)).toBe(nb('12,50 €'));
    expect(formatDishPrice({ kind: 'per_kg', priceCents: 4500 }, 'es', ES)).toBe(nb('45,00 €/kg'));
    expect(formatDishPrice({ kind: 'per_100g', priceCents: 600 }, 'es', ES)).toBe(nb('6,00 €/100 g'));
    expect(formatDishPrice({ kind: 'market', priceCents: null }, 'es', ES)).toBe('S/M');
    expect(formatDishPrice({ kind: 'per_kg', priceCents: 4500 }, 'en', EN)).toBe('€45.00/kg');
    expect(formatDishPrice({ kind: 'market', priceCents: null }, 'en', EN)).toBe('Market price');
  });

  it('parsePriceInput entiende cómo se escriben los precios en España', () => {
    expect(parsePriceInput('12,50')).toBe(1250);
    expect(parsePriceInput('12.5')).toBe(1250);
    expect(parsePriceInput('12')).toBe(1200);
    expect(parsePriceInput('0')).toBe(0);
    expect(parsePriceInput('0,29')).toBe(29);
    expect(parsePriceInput('1.250,00')).toBe(125000);
    expect(parsePriceInput('1.250')).toBe(125000);
    expect(parsePriceInput(' 12,50 € ')).toBe(1250);
    expect(parsePriceInput('99999,99')).toBe(9999999);
  });

  it('parsePriceInput rechaza lo que no es un importe válido', () => {
    for (const bad of ['', 'doce', '-1', '12,505', '100000', '1,2,3', '12.', '€']) expect(parsePriceInput(bad), bad).toBeNull();
  });

  it('centsToInput deja el importe listo para el formulario', () => {
    expect(centsToInput(1250)).toBe('12,50');
    expect(centsToInput(5)).toBe('0,05');
    expect(centsToInput(0)).toBe('0,00');
  });
});
```

Create `src/lib/menu/codes.ts`:

```ts
/** Los 14 alérgenos del Reglamento (UE) 1169/2011, anexo II, en el orden en que se muestran. Igual que la BD. */
export const ALLERGENS = [
  'gluten',
  'crustaceans',
  'eggs',
  'fish',
  'peanuts',
  'soy',
  'milk',
  'nuts',
  'celery',
  'mustard',
  'sesame',
  'sulphites',
  'lupin',
  'molluscs',
] as const;
export type Allergen = (typeof ALLERGENS)[number];

export const DISH_TAGS = ['vegan', 'vegetarian', 'gluten_free', 'spicy', 'new', 'recommended'] as const;
export type DishTag = (typeof DISH_TAGS)[number];

export const MENU_KINDS = ['carta', 'daily', 'special'] as const;
export type MenuKind = (typeof MENU_KINDS)[number];

export const PRICE_KINDS = ['fixed', 'per_kg', 'per_100g', 'market'] as const;
export type PriceKind = (typeof PRICE_KINDS)[number];

export const UNAVAILABLE_MODES = ['hide', 'strike'] as const;
export type UnavailableMode = (typeof UNAVAILABLE_MODES)[number];

/** Igual que los triggers de la BD (limit_menu_rows, check_menu_item, check_dish_prices). */
export const MENU_LIMITS = { menus: 20, sections: 40, items: 100, dishes: 500, prices: 4 } as const;
/** 99 999,99 €. */
export const MAX_PRICE_CENTS = 9_999_999;

/** Nombres para el panel. Los de la carta pública salen de messages.ts (en castellano, los mismos). */
export const ALLERGEN_LABELS_ES: Record<Allergen, string> = {
  gluten: 'Gluten',
  crustaceans: 'Crustáceos',
  eggs: 'Huevos',
  fish: 'Pescado',
  peanuts: 'Cacahuetes',
  soy: 'Soja',
  milk: 'Leche',
  nuts: 'Frutos de cáscara',
  celery: 'Apio',
  mustard: 'Mostaza',
  sesame: 'Sésamo',
  sulphites: 'Sulfitos',
  lupin: 'Altramuces',
  molluscs: 'Moluscos',
};

export const TAG_LABELS_ES: Record<DishTag, string> = {
  vegan: 'Vegano',
  vegetarian: 'Vegetariano',
  gluten_free: 'Sin gluten',
  spicy: 'Picante',
  new: 'Novedad',
  recommended: 'Recomendado',
};

export const MENU_KIND_LABELS_ES: Record<MenuKind, string> = {
  carta: 'Carta',
  daily: 'Menú del día',
  special: 'Menú especial',
};

export const PRICE_KIND_LABELS_ES: Record<PriceKind, string> = {
  fixed: 'Precio fijo',
  per_kg: 'Por kilo (€/kg)',
  per_100g: 'Por 100 g (€/100 g)',
  market: 'Según mercado (S/M)',
};

/** Alérgenos que impiden la etiqueta «Vegano» (igual que dishes_tags_allergens_check). */
export const VEGAN_BLOCKERS: readonly Allergen[] = ['milk', 'eggs', 'fish', 'crustaceans', 'molluscs'];

function oneOf<T extends string>(list: readonly T[]) {
  return (value: unknown): value is T => typeof value === 'string' && (list as readonly string[]).includes(value);
}

export const isAllergen = oneOf(ALLERGENS);
export const isDishTag = oneOf(DISH_TAGS);
export const isMenuKind = oneOf(MENU_KINDS);
export const isPriceKind = oneOf(PRICE_KINDS);
export const isUnavailableMode = oneOf(UNAVAILABLE_MODES);

/** Quita lo desconocido y los repetidos; orden de ALLERGENS. */
export function sortAllergens(values: readonly string[]): Allergen[] {
  return ALLERGENS.filter((a) => values.includes(a));
}

/** Quita lo desconocido y los repetidos; orden de DISH_TAGS. */
export function sortTags(values: readonly string[]): DishTag[] {
  return DISH_TAGS.filter((t) => values.includes(t));
}

/** La misma regla que la BD. null si se pueden combinar; si no, el mensaje para el panel. */
export function tagAllergenConflict(tags: readonly string[], allergens: readonly string[]): string | null {
  if (tags.includes('gluten_free') && allergens.includes('gluten')) {
    return 'Un plato con gluten no puede llevar la etiqueta «Sin gluten».';
  }
  if (tags.includes('vegan')) {
    const blocker = VEGAN_BLOCKERS.find((a) => allergens.includes(a));
    if (blocker) return `Un plato con ${ALLERGEN_LABELS_ES[blocker].toLowerCase()} no puede llevar la etiqueta «Vegano».`;
  }
  return null;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Escribir `i18n-text.ts`**

Create `src/lib/menu/i18n-text.ts`:

```ts
import { DEFAULT_LOCALE, isLocale, LOCALES, type Locale } from '@/lib/i18n/locales';

/** Texto escrito por el restaurante en cada idioma: {"es": "Croquetas", "en": "Croquettes"}. */
export type I18nText = Partial<Record<Locale, string>>;

/** El texto en el idioma pedido o, si falta, en castellano. null si no hay ninguno. */
export function textFor(value: I18nText | null | undefined, locale: Locale): string | null {
  const own = value?.[locale]?.trim();
  if (own) return own;
  return value?.[DEFAULT_LOCALE]?.trim() || null;
}

/** Idiomas activos sin traducción (para el aviso «Falta la traducción» del panel). */
export function missingLocales(value: I18nText | null | undefined, enabled: readonly Locale[]): Locale[] {
  return enabled.filter((l) => !value?.[l]?.trim());
}

/** Nombres de los campos de formulario de un texto: localeKeys('name') → ['name_es', 'name_en', …]. */
export function localeKeys<P extends string>(prefix: P): `${P}_${Locale}`[] {
  return LOCALES.map((l) => `${prefix}_${l}` as const);
}

/** Campos `${prefix}_${idioma}` de un formulario → texto por idioma, sin textos vacíos. */
export function i18nFromForm(values: Readonly<Record<string, string | undefined>>, prefix: string): I18nText {
  const out: I18nText = {};
  for (const l of LOCALES) {
    const value = values[`${prefix}_${l}`]?.trim();
    if (value) out[l] = value;
  }
  return out;
}

/** JSON de la BD → texto por idioma (solo idiomas conocidos y textos no vacíos). */
export function toI18nText(value: unknown): I18nText {
  const out: I18nText = {};
  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value)) return out;
  for (const [key, text] of Object.entries(value)) {
    if (isLocale(key) && typeof text === 'string' && text.trim()) out[key] = text;
  }
  return out;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Escribir `price.ts`**

Create `src/lib/menu/price.ts`:

```ts
import type { Locale } from '@/lib/i18n/locales';
import { MAX_PRICE_CENTS, type PriceKind } from './codes';

const NBSP = ' ';
const NUMBER_LOCALE: Record<Locale, string> = {
  es: 'es-ES',
  en: 'en-GB',
  ca: 'ca-ES',
  fr: 'fr-FR',
  de: 'de-DE',
  it: 'it-IT',
};

/** 1250 → '12,50 €' (con espacio duro), o '€12.50' en inglés. */
export function formatCents(cents: number, locale: Locale = 'es'): string {
  const amount = new Intl.NumberFormat(NUMBER_LOCALE[locale], { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 }).format(
    cents / 100,
  );
  return locale === 'en' ? `€${amount}` : `${amount}${NBSP}€`;
}

/** Textos de las unidades en el idioma de la página (salen de messages.ts). */
export type PriceUnitLabels = { perKg: string; per100g: string; market: string };

/** Un precio del plato tal como se muestra: '12,50 €', '45,00 €/kg', '6,00 €/100 g' o 'S/M'. */
export function formatDishPrice(
  price: { kind: PriceKind; priceCents: number | null },
  locale: Locale,
  units: PriceUnitLabels,
): string {
  if (price.kind === 'market' || price.priceCents === null) return units.market;
  const amount = formatCents(price.priceCents, locale);
  if (price.kind === 'per_kg') return `${amount}${units.perKg}`;
  if (price.kind === 'per_100g') return `${amount}${units.per100g}`;
  return amount;
}

/**
 * Importe escrito en el panel → céntimos: '12,50', '12.5', '12', '1.250,00', '1.250' (miles) o '12,50 €'.
 * null si no es un importe o se sale de 0–99 999,99.
 */
export function parsePriceInput(raw: string): number | null {
  const value = raw.replace(/€/g, '').replace(/\s/g, '');
  let normalized: string;
  if (/^\d{1,3}(\.\d{3})*,\d{1,2}$/.test(value) || /^\d+,\d{1,2}$/.test(value)) {
    normalized = value.replace(/\./g, '').replace(',', '.');
  } else if (/^\d{1,3}(\.\d{3})+$/.test(value)) {
    normalized = value.replace(/\./g, '');
  } else if (/^\d+(\.\d{1,2})?$/.test(value)) {
    normalized = value;
  } else {
    return null;
  }
  const cents = Math.round(Number(normalized) * 100);
  return Number.isFinite(cents) && cents >= 0 && cents <= MAX_PRICE_CENTS ? cents : null;
}

/** 1250 → '12,50' (valor inicial de los campos de importe). */
export function centsToInput(cents: number): string {
  return (cents / 100).toFixed(2).replace('.', ',');
}
```

### Task 6: Lógica pura: programación del menú del día y filtro de alérgenos

**Files:**
- Create: `src/lib/menu/schedule.ts`, `src/lib/menu/allergen-filter.ts`
- Test: `src/lib/menu/schedule.test.ts`, `src/lib/menu/allergen-filter.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `MINUTES_PER_DAY`, `formatRange`, `formatDateEs`, `isWeekday`, `WEEKDAY_LABELS_ES` de `src/lib/schedule.ts`; `type MenuKind`, `type Allergen` (Task 5).
- Produces:
  - `schedule.ts`: `type MenuSchedule = { kind: MenuKind; weekdays: number[] | null; startMinute: number | null; endMinute: number | null; dateFrom: string | null; dateTo: string | null }`; `type LocalMoment = { date: string; weekday: number; minute: number }`; `localMoment(now: Date, timeZone: string): LocalMoment`; `isMenuVisibleAt(s: MenuSchedule, now: Date, timeZone: string): boolean`; `visibleMenus<T extends MenuSchedule>(menus: readonly T[], now: Date, timeZone: string): T[]` (menús del día y especiales primero; cada grupo en su orden); `weekdaysSummaryEs(weekdays: readonly number[]): string`; `scheduleSummaryEs(s: MenuSchedule): string`.
  - `allergen-filter.ts`: `type FilterableItem = { allergens: readonly Allergen[] }`; `containsAny(item, excluded): boolean`; `filterSections<S extends { items: readonly FilterableItem[] }>(sections: readonly S[], excluded: readonly Allergen[]): { sections: S[]; hiddenCount: number }` (cada sección conserva su tipo; sus `items`, solo los que quedan); `needsAllergenNotice(item): boolean`.

Nota de fechas para las pruebas: el 30/11/2026 es lunes; el 04/12/2026, viernes. En 2026 el horario de verano empieza el 29/03 a la 01:00 UTC y acaba el 25/10 a la 01:00 UTC.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { isMenuVisibleAt, localMoment, scheduleSummaryEs, visibleMenus, weekdaysSummaryEs, type MenuSchedule } from './schedule';

const MADRID = 'Europe/Madrid';
const CANARY = 'Atlantic/Canary';
const at = (iso: string) => new Date(iso);

function daily(over: Partial<MenuSchedule> = {}): MenuSchedule {
  return { kind: 'daily', weekdays: [1, 2, 3, 4, 5], startMinute: 780, endMinute: 960, dateFrom: null, dateTo: null, ...over };
}

describe('localMoment', () => {
  it('fecha, día y minuto en la zona del restaurante, con el cambio de hora', () => {
    expect(localMoment(at('2026-11-30T12:30:00Z'), MADRID)).toEqual({ date: '2026-11-30', weekday: 1, minute: 810 });
    expect(localMoment(at('2026-11-30T12:30:00Z'), CANARY)).toEqual({ date: '2026-11-30', weekday: 1, minute: 750 });
    expect(localMoment(at('2026-07-01T12:30:00Z'), MADRID)).toEqual({ date: '2026-07-01', weekday: 3, minute: 870 });
    expect(localMoment(at('2026-03-29T01:30:00Z'), MADRID)).toEqual({ date: '2026-03-29', weekday: 7, minute: 210 });
    expect(localMoment(at('2026-11-30T23:30:00Z'), MADRID)).toEqual({ date: '2026-12-01', weekday: 2, minute: 30 });
    expect(localMoment(at('2026-11-30T23:30:00Z'), CANARY)).toEqual({ date: '2026-11-30', weekday: 1, minute: 1410 });
  });
});

describe('isMenuVisibleAt', () => {
  it('la carta siempre se ve', () => {
    const carta: MenuSchedule = { kind: 'carta', weekdays: null, startMinute: null, endMinute: null, dateFrom: null, dateTo: null };
    expect(isMenuVisibleAt(carta, at('2026-12-05T03:00:00Z'), MADRID)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('menú del día de lunes a viernes de 13:00 a 16:00 (la hora de fin ya no cuenta)', () => {
    const m = daily();
    expect(isMenuVisibleAt(m, at('2026-11-30T12:30:00Z'), MADRID)).toBe(true);
    expect(isMenuVisibleAt(m, at('2026-11-30T12:00:00Z'), MADRID)).toBe(true);
    expect(isMenuVisibleAt(m, at('2026-11-30T11:59:00Z'), MADRID)).toBe(false);
    expect(isMenuVisibleAt(m, at('2026-11-30T15:00:00Z'), MADRID)).toBe(false);
    expect(isMenuVisibleAt(m, at('2026-12-05T12:30:00Z'), MADRID)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('sin franja, todo el día de los días marcados', () => {
    const m = daily({ weekdays: [6, 7], startMinute: null, endMinute: null });
    expect(isMenuVisibleAt(m, at('2026-12-05T08:00:00Z'), MADRID)).toBe(true);
    expect(isMenuVisibleAt(m, at('2026-12-06T22:30:00Z'), MADRID)).toBe(true);
    expect(isMenuVisibleAt(m, at('2026-12-06T23:30:00Z'), MADRID)).toBe(false);
    expect(isMenuVisibleAt(m, at('2026-11-30T12:00:00Z'), MADRID)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('una franja que pasa de medianoche cuenta para el día en que empieza', () => {
    const m = daily({ weekdays: [5], startMinute: 1200, endMinute: 1560 });
    expect(isMenuVisibleAt(m, at('2026-12-04T22:00:00Z'), MADRID)).toBe(true);
    expect(isMenuVisibleAt(m, at('2026-12-05T00:30:00Z'), MADRID)).toBe(true);
    expect(isMenuVisibleAt(m, at('2026-12-05T01:30:00Z'), MADRID)).toBe(false);
    expect(isMenuVisibleAt(m, at('2026-12-04T00:30:00Z'), MADRID)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('fechas: el especial solo dentro de su rango, y la madrugada del último día cuenta para ese día', () => {
    const m: MenuSchedule = {
      kind: 'special',
      weekdays: [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7],
      startMinute: 1200,
      endMinute: 1500,
      dateFrom: '2026-12-24',
      dateTo: '2026-12-24',
    };
    expect(isMenuVisibleAt(m, at('2026-12-24T22:00:00Z'), MADRID)).toBe(true);
    expect(isMenuVisibleAt(m, at('2026-12-24T23:30:00Z'), MADRID)).toBe(true);
    expect(isMenuVisibleAt(m, at('2026-12-25T21:00:00Z'), MADRID)).toBe(false);
    expect(isMenuVisibleAt(m, at('2026-12-23T22:00:00Z'), MADRID)).toBe(false);
    const allDay = { ...m, startMinute: null, endMinute: null };
    expect(isMenuVisibleAt(allDay, at('2026-12-24T08:00:00Z'), MADRID)).toBe(true);
    expect(isMenuVisibleAt(allDay, at('2026-12-24T23:30:00Z'), MADRID)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('en Canarias va una hora por detrás', () => {
    const m = daily({ weekdays: [1] });
    expect(isMenuVisibleAt(m, at('2026-11-30T15:30:00Z'), MADRID)).toBe(false);
    expect(isMenuVisibleAt(m, at('2026-11-30T15:30:00Z'), CANARY)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('con el cambio de hora la franja sigue en hora local', () => {
    const m = daily({ weekdays: [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7], startMinute: 780, endMinute: 840 });
    expect(isMenuVisibleAt(m, at('2026-03-28T12:30:00Z'), MADRID)).toBe(true);
    expect(isMenuVisibleAt(m, at('2026-03-29T12:30:00Z'), MADRID)).toBe(false);
    expect(isMenuVisibleAt(m, at('2026-03-29T11:30:00Z'), MADRID)).toBe(true);
    expect(isMenuVisibleAt(m, at('2026-10-25T12:30:00Z'), MADRID)).toBe(true);
    expect(isMenuVisibleAt(m, at('2026-10-25T12:30:00Z'), CANARY)).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('visibleMenus', () => {
  it('primero los menús del día y especiales que se ven; después las cartas, cada grupo en su orden', () => {

    const menus = [

});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { Allergen } from './codes';
import { containsAny, filterSections, needsAllergenNotice } from './allergen-filter';

const item = (id: string, allergens: Allergen[]) => ({ id, allergens });
const sections = [
  { id: 'entrantes', title: 'Entrantes', items: [item('croquetas', ['gluten', 'milk']), item('ensalada', []), item('gambas', ['crustaceans'])] },
  { id: 'postres', title: 'Postres', items: [item('flan', ['eggs', 'milk'])] },
];

describe('filtro de alérgenos', () => {
  it('sin nada marcado no oculta nada', () => {
    const r = filterSections(sections, []);
    expect(r.hiddenCount).toBe(0);
    expect(r.sections.map((s) => s.items.map((i) => i.id))).toEqual([['croquetas', 'ensalada', 'gambas'], ['flan']]);
  });

  it('oculta los platos que llevan algo de lo marcado y cuenta cuántos quedan fuera', () => {
    const r = filterSections(sections, ['crustaceans', 'gluten']);
    expect(r.hiddenCount).toBe(2);
    expect(r.sections.map((s) => [s.id, s.items.map((i) => i.id)])).toEqual([
      ['entrantes', ['ensalada']],
      ['postres', ['flan']],
    ]);
    expect(r.sections[0].title).toBe('Entrantes');
  });

  it('las secciones que se quedan vacías desaparecen; los platos sin alérgenos marcados nunca se ocultan', () => {
    const r = filterSections(sections, ['milk', 'eggs', 'gluten', 'crustaceans']);
    expect(r.sections.map((s) => s.id)).toEqual(['entrantes']);
    expect(r.sections[0].items.map((i) => i.id)).toEqual(['ensalada']);
    expect(r.hiddenCount).toBe(3);
  });

  it('containsAny y needsAllergenNotice', () => {
    expect(containsAny(item('x', ['soy']), ['soy', 'milk'])).toBe(true);
    expect(containsAny(item('x', ['soy']), ['milk'])).toBe(false);
    expect(needsAllergenNotice(item('x', []))).toBe(true);
    expect(needsAllergenNotice(item('x', ['soy']))).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

Create `src/lib/menu/schedule.ts`:

```ts
import { formatDateEs, formatRange, isWeekday, MINUTES_PER_DAY, WEEKDAY_LABELS_ES } from '@/lib/schedule';
import type { MenuKind } from './codes';

/** Programación de una carta (la carta, «carta», no tiene). Días ISO 1 = lunes … 7 = domingo. */
export type MenuSchedule = {
  kind: MenuKind;
  weekdays: number[] | null;
  startMinute: number | null;
  endMinute: number | null;
  dateFrom: string | null;
  dateTo: string | null;
};

export type LocalMoment = { date: string; weekday: number; minute: number };

const WEEKDAY_INDEX: Record<string, number> = { Mon: 1, Tue: 2, Wed: 3, Thu: 4, Fri: 5, Sat: 6, Sun: 7 };

/** Fecha (AAAA-MM-DD), día ISO y minuto del día de un instante en una zona horaria (con el cambio de hora). */
export function localMoment(now: Date, timeZone: string): LocalMoment {
  const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', {
    timeZone,
    year: 'numeric',
    month: '2-digit',
    day: '2-digit',
    hour: '2-digit',
    minute: '2-digit',
    weekday: 'short',
    hourCycle: 'h23',
  }).formatToParts(now);
  const get = (type: Intl.DateTimeFormatPartTypes) => parts.find((p) => p.type === type)?.value ?? '';
  return {
    date: `${get('year')}-${get('month')}-${get('day')}`,
    weekday: WEEKDAY_INDEX[get('weekday')],
    minute: Number(get('hour')) * 60 + Number(get('minute')),
  };
}

function previousDate(iso: string): string {
  const [y, m, d] = iso.split('-').map(Number);
  return new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d - 1)).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
}

/** ¿Ese día (fecha y día de la semana) entra en la programación? */
function dayMatches(s: MenuSchedule, date: string, weekday: number): boolean {
  if (s.dateFrom && s.dateTo && (date < s.dateFrom || date > s.dateTo)) return false;
  return (s.weekdays ?? []).includes(weekday);
}

/**
 * ¿Se ve ahora? La carta, siempre. El menú del día y el especial, si hoy (en la zona del restaurante) está en sus
 * fechas, es uno de sus días y la hora está en su franja (de inicio incluido a fin excluido). Una franja que pasa
 * de medianoche cuenta para el día en que empieza.
 */
export function isMenuVisibleAt(s: MenuSchedule, now: Date, timeZone: string): boolean {
  if (s.kind === 'carta') return true;
  const t = localMoment(now, timeZone);
  if (s.startMinute === null || s.endMinute === null) return dayMatches(s, t.date, t.weekday);
  if (dayMatches(s, t.date, t.weekday) && t.minute >= s.startMinute && t.minute < s.endMinute) return true;
  // De madrugada: la franja de ayer que pasa de medianoche.
  if (s.endMinute > MINUTES_PER_DAY && t.minute < s.endMinute - MINUTES_PER_DAY) {
    const yesterday = t.weekday === 1 ? 7 : t.weekday - 1;
    return dayMatches(s, previousDate(t.date), yesterday);
  }
  return false;
}

/** Cartas que se ven ahora: primero los menús del día y especiales, luego las cartas; cada grupo, en su orden. */
export function visibleMenus<T extends MenuSchedule>(menus: readonly T[], now: Date, timeZone: string): T[] {
  const visible = menus.filter((m) => isMenuVisibleAt(m, now, timeZone));
  return [...visible.filter((m) => m.kind !== 'carta'), ...visible.filter((m) => m.kind === 'carta')];
}

const capitalize = (text: string) => text.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + text.slice(1);

/** [1,2,3,4,5] → 'De lunes a viernes'; [6,7] → 'Sábado y domingo'; [1,3,5] → 'Lunes, miércoles y viernes'. */
export function weekdaysSummaryEs(weekdays: readonly number[]): string {
  const days = [...new Set(weekdays)].filter(isWeekday).sort((a, b) => a - b);
  if (days.length === 7) return 'Todos los días';
  if (days.length === 0) return 'Ningún día';
  const names = days.map((d) => WEEKDAY_LABELS_ES[d].toLowerCase());
  const consecutive = days.length >= 3 && days.every((d, i) => i === 0 || d === days[i - 1] + 1);
  if (consecutive) return `De ${names[0]} a ${names[names.length - 1]}`;
  if (names.length === 1) return capitalize(names[0]);
  return capitalize(`${names.slice(0, -1).join(', ')} y ${names[names.length - 1]}`);
}

/** Para el panel: 'De lunes a viernes · 13:00–16:00 · Del 01/12/2026 al 31/12/2026'. */
export function scheduleSummaryEs(s: MenuSchedule): string {
  if (s.kind === 'carta') return 'Siempre visible';
  const parts = [weekdaysSummaryEs(s.weekdays ?? [])];
  parts.push(s.startMinute !== null && s.endMinute !== null ? formatRange(s.startMinute, s.endMinute) : 'Todo el día');
  if (s.dateFrom && s.dateTo) {
    parts.push(s.dateFrom === s.dateTo ? `El ${formatDateEs(s.dateFrom)}` : `Del ${formatDateEs(s.dateFrom)} al ${formatDateEs(s.dateTo)}`);
  }
  return parts.join(' · ');
}
```

Create `src/lib/menu/allergen-filter.ts`:

```ts
import type { Allergen } from './codes';

export type FilterableItem = { allergens: readonly Allergen[] };

/** ¿Lleva alguno de los alérgenos que el comensal ha marcado? */
export function containsAny(item: FilterableItem, excluded: readonly Allergen[]): boolean {
  return item.allergens.some((a) => excluded.includes(a));
}

/**
 * Oculta los platos con alguno de los alérgenos marcados. Los platos sin ningún alérgeno marcado se quedan
 * (con el aviso de preguntar al personal). Las secciones que se quedan vacías desaparecen.
 * Solo en el navegador: no se guarda ni se envía nada.
 */
export function filterSections<S extends { items: readonly FilterableItem[] }>(
  sections: readonly S[],
  excluded: readonly Allergen[],
): { sections: S[]; hiddenCount: number } {
  let hiddenCount = 0;
  const out: S[] = [];
  for (const section of sections) {
    const items = section.items.filter((item) => {
      const hide = excluded.length > 0 && containsAny(item, excluded);
      if (hide) hiddenCount += 1;
      return !hide;
    });
    if (items.length > 0) out.push({ ...section, items });
  }
  return { sections: out, hiddenCount };
}

/** Sin alérgenos marcados hay que preguntar al personal. */
export function needsAllergenNotice(item: FilterableItem): boolean {
  return item.allergens.length === 0;
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { ALLERGEN_LABELS_ES, ALLERGENS, DISH_TAGS, TAG_LABELS_ES } from '@/lib/menu/codes';
import { LOCALES } from './locales';
import { getMessages, MESSAGES, QR_CARD_TEXT } from './messages';

describe('mensajes de la mini-web', () => {
  it('hay mensajes para los seis idiomas, con siete días y sin textos vacíos', () => {
    expect(Object.keys(MESSAGES).sort()).toEqual([...LOCALES].sort());
    for (const locale of LOCALES) {
      const m = getMessages(locale);
      expect(m.weekdays, locale).toHaveLength(7);
      for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(m)) {
        if (key === 'legalSpanishOnly' && locale === 'es') continue;
        if (typeof value === 'string') expect(value.trim(), `${locale}.${key}`).not.toBe('');
      }
    }
  });

    expect([es.marketPrice, es.perKg, es.per100g]).toEqual(['S/M', '/kg', '/100 g']);
    expect(getMessages('en').menuTitle('La Taberna')).toBe('La Taberna menu');
    expect(getMessages('de').filterHidden(2)).toBe('2 Gerichte durch Ihren Filter ausgeblendet.');
    expect(getMessages('ca').unavailable).toBe('Exhaurit');
    expect(QR_CARD_TEXT).toBe('Escanea para ver la carta · Scan to see the menu');
  });
});
```

Create `src/components/menu/AllergenIcon.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { renderToStaticMarkup } from 'react-dom/server';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { ALLERGENS } from '@/lib/menu/codes';
import { AllergenIcon, AllergenLabel } from './AllergenIcon';

describe('iconos de alérgenos', () => {
  it('hay un pictograma propio para cada uno de los 14, decorativo para los lectores de pantalla', () => {
    const drawings = new Set<string>();
    for (const allergen of ALLERGENS) {
      const html = renderToStaticMarkup(<AllergenIcon allergen={allergen} />);
      expect(html, allergen).toMatch(/^<svg[^>]*aria-hidden="true"/);
      expect(html, allergen).toMatch(/<(path|circle|rect|text)\b/);
      drawings.add(html);
    }
    expect(drawings.size).toBe(14);
  });

  it('la etiqueta siempre lleva el nombre del alérgeno', () => {
    const html = renderToStaticMarkup(<AllergenLabel allergen="sulphites" name="Sulfitos" />);
    expect(html).toContain('Sulfitos');
    expect(html).toContain('aria-hidden="true"');
  });
});
```

```ts
};

  per100g: '/100\u00a0g',
  vatIncluded: 'Prezzi IVA inclusa',

    'Queste informazioni sono indicative. Se hai un’allergia o un’intolleranza, chiedi conferma al personale prima di ordinare.',

  filterClear: 'Togli il filtro',

  dishPhotoAlt: (dish) => `Foto di ${dish}`,
  enlargePhoto: (dish) => `Ingrandisci la foto di ${dish}`,
  closePhoto: 'Chiudi',
};

export const MESSAGES: Record<Locale, PublicMessages> = { es, en, ca, fr, de, it };

/** Texto fijo de las tarjetas QR impresas (no depende del idioma: siempre castellano e inglés). */
export const QR_CARD_TEXT = 'Escanea para ver la carta · Scan to see the menu';

export function getMessages(locale: Locale): PublicMessages {
  return MESSAGES[locale];
}
```

```tsx
};

export function AllergenIcon({
  allergen,
  className = 'size-5 shrink-0',
  ...props
}: { allergen: Allergen } & Omit<SVGProps<SVGSVGElement>, 'children'>) {
  return (
    <svg
      viewBox="0 0 24 24"
      aria-hidden="true"
      focusable="false"
      fill="none"
      stroke="currentColor"
      strokeWidth={1.75}
      strokeLinecap="round"
      strokeLinejoin="round"
      className={className}
      {...props}
    >
      {ICONS[allergen]}
    </svg>
  );
}

/** Icono y nombre del alérgeno: el icono nunca va solo. */
export function AllergenLabel({ allergen, name, className = '' }: { allergen: Allergen; name: string; className?: string }) {
  return (
    <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 ${className}`}>
      <AllergenIcon allergen={allergen} />
      <span>{name}</span>
    </span>
  );
}
```

### Task 8: Validación de los formularios, errores de BD, navegación y fotos de platos

**Files:**
- Create: `src/lib/menu/validation.ts`
- Test: `src/lib/menu/validation.test.ts`
- Modify: `src/lib/validation/schedule.ts` (exportar `isRealDate`), `src/lib/db-errors.ts` + `src/lib/db-errors.test.ts`, `src/lib/panel-nav.ts` + `src/lib/panel-nav.test.ts`, `src/lib/media.ts` + `src/lib/media.test.ts`, `src/lib/images/compress.ts` + `src/lib/images/compress.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 5 (`codes.ts`, `i18n-text.ts`, `price.ts`); `formStrings`, `fieldErrorsFrom` de `src/lib/forms.ts`; `daysBetween`, `timeToMinutes`, `toServiceRange` de `src/lib/schedule.ts`.
- Produces:
  - `validation.ts`: `MENU_TEXT_KEYS`, `SECTION_TEXT_KEYS`, `INLINE_TEXT_KEYS`, `DISH_TEXT_KEYS`, `PRICE_ROWS = [0, 1, 2, 3]`; lectores de `FormData` → entrada del esquema: `readMenuForm(fd)`, `readSectionForm(fd)`, `readDishForm(fd)`, `readInlineItemForm(fd)`; esquemas `menuFormSchema` (→ `MenuFormValues = { kind; active; names; includes; priceCents; weekdays; startMinute; endMinute; dateFrom; dateTo }`), `sectionFormSchema` (→ `{ names }`), `dishFormSchema` (→ `DishFormValues = { names; descriptions; allergens; tags; prices: { kind; priceCents; labels }[] }`), `inlineItemSchema` (→ `{ names; allergens }`), `unavailableModeSchema`, `confirmSchema` (`{ confirm: 'on' }`). Nombres de campo: `kind`, `active` (`on`), `price`, `weekdays` (varios), `start`, `end`, `dateFrom`, `dateTo`, `name_<idioma>`, `includes_<idioma>`, `description_<idioma>`, `allergens` (varios), `tags` (varios), `price<n>_kind`, `price<n>_amount`, `price<n>_label_<idioma>`, `confirm`. Los errores salen con la clave del campo (para `fieldErrorsFrom`).
  - `src/lib/validation/schedule.ts`: `export function isRealDate(iso: string): boolean`.
  - `db-errors.ts`: mensajes para `MENU_LIMIT`, `SECTION_LIMIT`, `ITEM_LIMIT`, `DISH_LIMIT`, `PRICE_LIMIT`, `MARKET_PRICE_ALONE`, `INLINE_ONLY_DAILY`, `menu_items_menu_dish_key`, `dishes_tags_allergens_check`, `dish_prices_amount_check`, `menus_*_check` y `*_names_check`.
  - `panel-nav.ts`: «Menú» (`/menu`, permiso `menu.toggle_unavailable`) entre «Local» y «Horario».
  - `media.ts`: `dishPhotoPath(restaurantId, ext, id): string`, `isDishPhotoPath(restaurantId, path): boolean`.
  - `compress.ts`: `type ImageTarget = MediaKind | 'dish'`; `MAX_SIDE.dish = 1200`; `compressImage(file, kind: ImageTarget)`.

Lee antes: `src/lib/validation/schedule.ts` (patrón de `transform` con `ctx.addIssue` y `z.NEVER`) y `src/lib/forms.ts`. En Zod 4, una incidencia añadida en `transform` hace fallar el `safeParse`; aun así devolvemos `z.NEVER` para no construir un valor a medias.

- [ ] **Step 1: Escribir las pruebas de validación (fallan)**

Create `src/lib/menu/validation.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { fieldErrorsFrom } from '@/lib/forms';
import {
  confirmSchema,
  dishFormSchema,
  inlineItemSchema,
  menuFormSchema,
  readDishForm,
  readInlineItemForm,
  readMenuForm,
  readSectionForm,
  sectionFormSchema,
  unavailableModeSchema,
} from './validation';

function form(entries: [string, string][]): FormData {
  const fd = new FormData();
  for (const [key, value] of entries) fd.append(key, value);
  return fd;
}

function errors(result: { success: boolean; error?: Parameters<typeof fieldErrorsFrom>[0] }) {
  expect(result.success).toBe(false);
  return result.error ? fieldErrorsFrom(result.error) : {};
}

const noSchedule = { includes: {}, priceCents: null, weekdays: null, startMinute: null, endMinute: null, dateFrom: null, dateTo: null };

describe('formulario de carta', () => {
  it('una carta solo necesita el nombre en castellano; lo del menú del día se ignora', () => {
    const r = menuFormSchema.safeParse(
      readMenuForm(form([['kind', 'carta'], ['name_es', ' Carta '], ['name_en', 'Menu'], ['active', 'on'], ['price', '12'], ['weekdays', '1']])),
    );
    expect(r.success && r.data).toEqual({ kind: 'carta', active: true, names: { es: 'Carta', en: 'Menu' }, ...noSchedule });
  });

  it('sin nombre en castellano o con un nombre largo, el error sale junto al campo', () => {
    const r = menuFormSchema.safeParse(readMenuForm(form([['kind', 'carta'], ['name_en', 'x'.repeat(61)]])));
    expect(errors(r)).toEqual({
      name_en: 'El nombre puede tener como mucho 60 caracteres.',
      name_es: 'Ponle nombre a la carta (por ejemplo, Carta o Menú del día).',
    });
  });

  it('menú del día con precio, días, «incluye» y una franja que pasa de medianoche', () => {
    const r = menuFormSchema.safeParse(
      readMenuForm(
        form([
          ['kind', 'daily'],
          ['name_es', 'Menú del día'],
          ['price', '15,50'],
          ['weekdays', '5'],
          ['weekdays', '1'],
          ['weekdays', '1'],
          ['start', '20:00'],
          ['end', '02:00'],
          ['includes_es', 'Pan, bebida y postre'],
        ]),
      ),
    );
    expect(r.success && r.data).toEqual({
      kind: 'daily',
      active: false,
      names: { es: 'Menú del día' },
      includes: { es: 'Pan, bebida y postre' },
      priceCents: 1550,
      weekdays: [1, 5],
      startMinute: 1200,
      endMinute: 1560,
      dateFrom: null,
      dateTo: null,
    });
  });

  it('menú del día sin precio, sin días y con una sola hora', () => {
    const r = menuFormSchema.safeParse(readMenuForm(form([['kind', 'daily'], ['name_es', 'Menú'], ['price', 'quince'], ['start', '13:00']])));
    expect(errors(r)).toEqual({
      price: 'Escribe el precio del menú (por ejemplo, 15,50).',
      weekdays: 'Elige al menos un día.',
      end: 'Pon la hora de inicio y la de fin, o deja las dos vacías.',
    });
  });

  it('menú especial: fechas obligatorias, un solo día si falta la final y fechas que existan', () => {
    const base: [string, string][] = [['kind', 'special'], ['name_es', 'Nochebuena'], ['price', '45'], ['weekdays', '4']];
    expect(errors(menuFormSchema.safeParse(readMenuForm(form(base))))).toEqual({ dateFrom: 'Elige las fechas del menú especial.' });
    const oneDay = menuFormSchema.safeParse(readMenuForm(form([...base, ['dateFrom', '2026-12-24']])));
    expect(oneDay.success && [oneDay.data.dateFrom, oneDay.data.dateTo]).toEqual(['2026-12-24', '2026-12-24']);
    expect(errors(menuFormSchema.safeParse(readMenuForm(form([...base, ['dateFrom', '2026-02-31']]))))).toEqual({
      dateFrom: 'Esa fecha no existe.',
    });
    expect(
      errors(menuFormSchema.safeParse(readMenuForm(form([...base, ['dateFrom', '2026-12-24'], ['dateTo', '2026-12-20']])))),
    ).toEqual({ dateTo: 'La fecha final no puede ser anterior a la inicial.' });
  });
});

describe('formulario de plato', () => {
  it('textos, alérgenos y etiquetas en orden y dos precios con etiqueta; las filas vacías no cuentan', () => {
    const r = dishFormSchema.safeParse(
      readDishForm(
        form([
          ['name_es', 'Croquetas'],
          ['description_es', 'De jamón'],
          ['allergens', 'milk'],
          ['allergens', 'gluten'],
          ['allergens', 'pollen'],
          ['tags', 'recommended'],
          ['price0_kind', 'fixed'],
          ['price0_amount', '9'],
          ['price0_label_es', 'Media'],
          ['price1_kind', 'fixed'],
          ['price1_amount', '15,00'],
          ['price1_label_es', 'Ración'],
          ['price1_label_en', 'Portion'],
          ['price2_kind', 'fixed'],
          ['price2_amount', ''],
          ['price3_kind', 'per_kg'],
          ['price3_amount', ''],
        ]),
      ),
    );
    expect(r.success && r.data).toEqual({
      names: { es: 'Croquetas' },
      descriptions: { es: 'De jamón' },
      allergens: ['gluten', 'milk'],
      tags: ['recommended'],
      prices: [
        { kind: 'fixed', priceCents: 900, labels: { es: 'Media' } },
        { kind: 'fixed', priceCents: 1500, labels: { es: 'Ración', en: 'Portion' } },
      ],
    });
  });

  it('«según mercado» va solo y sin importe', () => {
    const ok = dishFormSchema.safeParse(readDishForm(form([['name_es', 'Pescado del día'], ['price0_kind', 'market'], ['price0_amount', '12']])));
    expect(ok.success && ok.data.prices).toEqual([{ kind: 'market', priceCents: null, labels: {} }]);
    const bad = dishFormSchema.safeParse(
      readDishForm(form([['name_es', 'Pescado'], ['price0_kind', 'fixed'], ['price0_amount', '12'], ['price1_kind', 'market']])),
    );
    expect(errors(bad)).toEqual({ price1_kind: 'Un precio «según mercado» va solo: deja vacías las demás filas.' });
  });

  it('importes que no valen, etiqueta sin importe, etiquetas incompatibles y sin nombre', () => {
    const r = dishFormSchema.safeParse(
      readDishForm(
        form([
          ['price0_kind', 'fixed'],
          ['price0_amount', 'doce'],
          ['price1_kind', 'fixed'],
          ['price1_label_es', 'Media'],
          ['allergens', 'milk'],
          ['tags', 'vegan'],
        ]),
      ),
    );
    expect(errors(r)).toEqual({
      name_es: 'Ponle nombre al plato.',
      tags: 'Un plato con leche no puede llevar la etiqueta «Vegano».',
      price0_amount: 'Escribe un importe entre 0 y 99.999,99 (por ejemplo, 12,50).',
      price1_amount: 'Escribe el importe o deja la fila vacía.',
    });
  });

  it('un plato sin precios también vale', () => {
    const r = dishFormSchema.safeParse(readDishForm(form([['name_es', 'Pan']])));
    expect(r.success && r.data.prices).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe('sección, plato escrito a mano y ajustes', () => {
  it('la sección necesita nombre en castellano', () => {
    expect(errors(sectionFormSchema.safeParse(readSectionForm(form([['name_en', 'Starters']]))))).toEqual({
      name_es: 'Ponle nombre a la sección (por ejemplo, Entrantes).',
    });
    const ok = sectionFormSchema.safeParse(readSectionForm(form([['name_es', 'Entrantes'], ['name_en', 'Starters']])));
    expect(ok.success && ok.data).toEqual({ names: { es: 'Entrantes', en: 'Starters' } });
  });

  it('el plato escrito a mano: nombre y alérgenos en orden', () => {
    const ok = inlineItemSchema.safeParse(
      readInlineItemForm(form([['name_es', 'Sopa de pescado'], ['allergens', 'celery'], ['allergens', 'fish']])),
    );
    expect(ok.success && ok.data).toEqual({ names: { es: 'Sopa de pescado' }, allergens: ['fish', 'celery'] });
    expect(errors(inlineItemSchema.safeParse(readInlineItemForm(form([]))))).toEqual({ name_es: 'Ponle nombre al plato.' });
  });

  it('modo de agotados y confirmación de borrado', () => {
    expect(unavailableModeSchema.safeParse('hide').success).toBe(true);
    expect(unavailableModeSchema.safeParse('blur').success).toBe(false);
    expect(confirmSchema.safeParse({ confirm: 'on' }).success).toBe(true);
    expect(errors(confirmSchema.safeParse({ confirm: '' }))).toEqual({ confirm: 'Marca la casilla para confirmar.' });
  });
});
```
