- [ ] **Step 5: Escribir `validation.ts`**

Create `src/lib/menu/validation.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from 'zod';
import { formStrings } from '@/lib/forms';
import { LOCALES } from '@/lib/i18n/locales';
import { daysBetween, timeToMinutes, toServiceRange } from '@/lib/schedule';
import { isRealDate } from '@/lib/validation/schedule';
import {
  MENU_KINDS,
  PRICE_KINDS,
  sortAllergens,
  sortTags,
  tagAllergenConflict,
  UNAVAILABLE_MODES,
  type Allergen,
  type DishTag,
  type MenuKind,
  type PriceKind,
} from './codes';
import { localeKeys, type I18nText } from './i18n-text';
import { parsePriceInput } from './price';

const ISO_DATE = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/;
const NO_SUCH_DATE = 'Esa fecha no existe.';
const BAD_TIME = 'Escribe la hora así: 13:30.';
const BAD_RANGE = 'Revisa las horas: tiene que acabar después de empezar y, como mucho, a las 06:00 del día siguiente.';

type Issue = { code: 'custom'; path: string[]; message: string };
type Ctx = { addIssue: (issue: Issue) => void };

/** Cuenta las incidencias para devolver z.NEVER en cuanto haya alguna. */
function tracking(ctx: Ctx) {
  let count = 0;
  return {
    ctx: {
      addIssue: (issue: Issue) => {
        count += 1;
        ctx.addIssue(issue);
      },
    },
    failed: () => count > 0,
  };
}

/** `${prefix}_${idioma}` → texto por idioma, con longitud máxima y, si se pide, castellano obligatorio. */
function readI18n(
  texts: Record<string, string>,
  prefix: string,
  ctx: Ctx,
  opts: { max: number; tooLong: string; required?: string },
): I18nText {
  const out: I18nText = {};
  for (const l of LOCALES) {
    const key = `${prefix}_${l}`;
    const value = (texts[key] ?? '').trim();
    if (value.length > opts.max) ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: [key], message: opts.tooLong });
    else if (value) out[l] = value;
  }
  const es = (texts[`${prefix}_es`] ?? '').trim();
  if (opts.required && es === '') ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: [`${prefix}_es`], message: opts.required });
  return out;
}

const texts = z.record(z.string(), z.string());

// Cartas ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
export const MENU_TEXT_KEYS = [...localeKeys('name'), ...localeKeys('includes')];

export function readMenuForm(fd: FormData) {
  return {
    ...formStrings(fd, ['kind', 'price', 'start', 'end', 'dateFrom', 'dateTo'] as const),
    active: fd.get('active') === 'on',
    weekdays: fd.getAll('weekdays').map(String),
    texts: formStrings(fd, MENU_TEXT_KEYS),
  };
}

export type MenuFormValues = {
  kind: MenuKind;
  active: boolean;
  names: I18nText;
  includes: I18nText;
  priceCents: number | null;
  weekdays: number[] | null;
  startMinute: number | null;
  endMinute: number | null;
  dateFrom: string | null;
  dateTo: string | null;
};

/** Crear o editar una carta. En «carta» se ignoran precio y programación; en «daily»/«special» son obligatorios. */
export const menuFormSchema = z
  .object({
    kind: z.enum(MENU_KINDS, { error: 'Elige qué tipo de carta es.' }),
    active: z.boolean(),
    price: z.string(),
    start: z.string(),
    end: z.string(),
    dateFrom: z.string(),
    dateTo: z.string(),
    weekdays: z.array(z.string()),
    texts,
  })
  .transform((v, rawCtx): MenuFormValues => {
    const t = tracking(rawCtx);
    const names = readI18n(v.texts, 'name', t.ctx, {
      max: 60,
      tooLong: 'El nombre puede tener como mucho 60 caracteres.',
      required: 'Ponle nombre a la carta (por ejemplo, Carta o Menú del día).',
    });
    if (v.kind === 'carta') {
      if (t.failed()) return z.NEVER;
      return { kind: v.kind, active: v.active, names, includes: {}, priceCents: null, weekdays: null, startMinute: null, endMinute: null, dateFrom: null, dateTo: null };
    }
    const includes = readI18n(v.texts, 'includes', t.ctx, { max: 300, tooLong: 'Como mucho 300 caracteres.' });
    const priceCents = parsePriceInput(v.price);
    if (priceCents === null) t.ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['price'], message: 'Escribe el precio del menú (por ejemplo, 15,50).' });
    const weekdays = [...new Set(v.weekdays.map(Number))].filter((d) => Number.isInteger(d) && d >= 1 && d <= 7).sort((a, b) => a - b);
    if (weekdays.length === 0) t.ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['weekdays'], message: 'Elige al menos un día.' });

    let startMinute: number | null = null;
    let endMinute: number | null = null;
    const start = v.start.trim();
    const end = v.end.trim();
    if (start || end) {
      if (!start || !end) {
        t.ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: [start ? 'end' : 'start'], message: 'Pon la hora de inicio y la de fin, o deja las dos vacías.' });
      } else if (timeToMinutes(start) === null) {
        t.ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['start'], message: BAD_TIME });
      } else if (timeToMinutes(end) === null) {
        t.ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['end'], message: BAD_TIME });
      } else {
        const range = toServiceRange(start, end);
        if (!range) t.ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['end'], message: BAD_RANGE });
        else ({ startMinute, endMinute } = range);
      }
    }

    let dateFrom: string | null = null;
    let dateTo: string | null = null;
    const from = v.dateFrom.trim();
    const to = v.dateTo.trim() || from;
    if (from) {
      if (!ISO_DATE.test(from) || !isRealDate(from)) t.ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['dateFrom'], message: NO_SUCH_DATE });
      else if (!ISO_DATE.test(to) || !isRealDate(to)) t.ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['dateTo'], message: NO_SUCH_DATE });
      else if (to < from) t.ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['dateTo'], message: 'La fecha final no puede ser anterior a la inicial.' });
      else if (daysBetween(from, to) > 366) t.ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['dateTo'], message: 'Como mucho, un año seguido.' });
      else [dateFrom, dateTo] = [from, to];
    } else if (v.dateTo.trim()) {
      t.ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['dateFrom'], message: 'Elige también la fecha de inicio.' });
    } else if (v.kind === 'special') {
      t.ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['dateFrom'], message: 'Elige las fechas del menú especial.' });
    }

    if (t.failed()) return z.NEVER;
    return { kind: v.kind, active: v.active, names, includes, priceCents, weekdays, startMinute, endMinute, dateFrom, dateTo };
  });

// Secciones -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
export const SECTION_TEXT_KEYS = localeKeys('name');

export function readSectionForm(fd: FormData) {
  return { texts: formStrings(fd, SECTION_TEXT_KEYS) };
}

export const sectionFormSchema = z.object({ texts }).transform((v, rawCtx): { names: I18nText } => {
  const t = tracking(rawCtx);
  const names = readI18n(v.texts, 'name', t.ctx, {
    max: 60,
    tooLong: 'El nombre puede tener como mucho 60 caracteres.',
    required: 'Ponle nombre a la sección (por ejemplo, Entrantes).',
  });
  if (t.failed()) return z.NEVER;
  return { names };
});

// Platos ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
/** Filas de precio del formulario (como mucho 4, igual que la BD). */
export const PRICE_ROWS = [0, 1, 2, 3] as const;
export const DISH_TEXT_KEYS = [
  ...localeKeys('name'),
  ...localeKeys('description'),
  ...PRICE_ROWS.flatMap((n) => localeKeys(`price${n}_label`)),
];

export function readDishForm(fd: FormData) {
  return {
    allergens: fd.getAll('allergens').map(String),
    tags: fd.getAll('tags').map(String),
    prices: PRICE_ROWS.map((n) => {
      const kind = fd.get(`price${n}_kind`);
      const amount = fd.get(`price${n}_amount`);
      return { kind: typeof kind === 'string' ? kind : 'fixed', amount: typeof amount === 'string' ? amount : '' };
    }),
    texts: formStrings(fd, DISH_TEXT_KEYS),
  };
}

export type DishFormValues = {
  names: I18nText;
  descriptions: I18nText;
  allergens: Allergen[];
  tags: DishTag[];
  prices: { kind: PriceKind; priceCents: number | null; labels: I18nText }[];
};

export const dishFormSchema = z
  .object({
    allergens: z.array(z.string()),
    tags: z.array(z.string()),
    prices: z.array(z.object({ kind: z.string(), amount: z.string() })).max(PRICE_ROWS.length),
    texts,
  })
  .transform((v, rawCtx): DishFormValues => {
    const t = tracking(rawCtx);
    const names = readI18n(v.texts, 'name', t.ctx, {
      max: 120,
      tooLong: 'El nombre puede tener como mucho 120 caracteres.',
      required: 'Ponle nombre al plato.',
    });
    const descriptions = readI18n(v.texts, 'description', t.ctx, {
      max: 500,
      tooLong: 'La descripción puede tener como mucho 500 caracteres.',
    });
    const allergens = sortAllergens(v.allergens);
    const tags = sortTags(v.tags);
    const conflict = tagAllergenConflict(tags, allergens);
    if (conflict) t.ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['tags'], message: conflict });

    const prices: DishFormValues['prices'] = [];
    v.prices.forEach((row, n) => {
      const kind: PriceKind = (PRICE_KINDS as readonly string[]).includes(row.kind) ? (row.kind as PriceKind) : 'fixed';
      const labels = readI18n(v.texts, `price${n}_label`, t.ctx, { max: 40, tooLong: 'La etiqueta puede tener como mucho 40 caracteres.' });
      if (kind === 'market') {
        prices.push({ kind, priceCents: null, labels });
        return;
      }
      const amount = row.amount.trim();
      if (!amount) {
        if (Object.keys(labels).length > 0) {
          t.ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: [`price${n}_amount`], message: 'Escribe el importe o deja la fila vacía.' });
        }
        return;
      }
      const priceCents = parsePriceInput(amount);
      if (priceCents === null) {
        t.ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: [`price${n}_amount`], message: 'Escribe un importe entre 0 y 99.999,99 (por ejemplo, 12,50).' });
        return;
      }
      prices.push({ kind, priceCents, labels });
    });
    const marketRow = v.prices.findIndex((row) => row.kind === 'market');
    if (marketRow >= 0 && prices.length > 1) {
      t.ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: [`price${marketRow}_kind`], message: 'Un precio «según mercado» va solo: deja vacías las demás filas.' });
    }

    if (t.failed()) return z.NEVER;
    return { names, descriptions, allergens, tags, prices };
  });

// Platos escritos solo para un menú del día o especial ---------------------------------------------------------
export const INLINE_TEXT_KEYS = localeKeys('name');

export function readInlineItemForm(fd: FormData) {
  return { allergens: fd.getAll('allergens').map(String), texts: formStrings(fd, INLINE_TEXT_KEYS) };
}

export const inlineItemSchema = z
  .object({ allergens: z.array(z.string()), texts })
  .transform((v, rawCtx): { names: I18nText; allergens: Allergen[] } => {
    const t = tracking(rawCtx);
    const names = readI18n(v.texts, 'name', t.ctx, {
      max: 120,
      tooLong: 'El nombre puede tener como mucho 120 caracteres.',
      required: 'Ponle nombre al plato.',
    });
    if (t.failed()) return z.NEVER;
    return { names, allergens: sortAllergens(v.allergens) };
  });

// Ajustes ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
export const unavailableModeSchema = z.enum(UNAVAILABLE_MODES, { error: 'Elige cómo se ven los platos agotados.' });

/** Casilla «Sí, quiero borrarlo» de los formularios de borrado. */
export const confirmSchema = z.object({ confirm: z.literal('on', { error: 'Marca la casilla para confirmar.' }) });
```

```ts
/** Foto de un plato: {restaurante}/dishes/{id}.webp. La gestionan Administrador y Encargado (can_manage_media). */
export function dishPhotoPath(restaurantId: string, ext: 'webp' | 'jpg', id: string): string {
  return `${restaurantId}/dishes/${id}.${ext}`;
}

/** Misma regla que can_manage_media y dishes_photo_path_check. */
export function isDishPhotoPath(restaurantId: string, path: string): boolean {
  if (!new RegExp(`^${UUID}$`).test(restaurantId)) return false;
  return new RegExp(`^${restaurantId}/dishes/[a-z0-9-]+\\.(webp|jpg)$`).test(path);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Carta de ejemplo para las pruebas**

Create `src/lib/public/menu-fixtures.ts`:

```ts
const RID = '11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111';

const croquetas = {
  id: 'd-croquetas',
  names: { es: 'Croquetas', en: 'Croquettes' },
  descriptions: { es: 'De jamón ibérico.' },
  photo_path: `${RID}/dishes/croquetas.webp`,
  allergens: ['milk', 'gluten'],
  tags: ['recommended'],
  unavailable: false,
  prices: [
    { kind: 'fixed', price_cents: 900, labels: { es: 'Media', en: 'Half' } },
    { kind: 'fixed', price_cents: 1500, labels: { es: 'Ración' } },
  ],
};
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { SAMPLE_MENU_JSON } from './menu-fixtures';
import { parsePublicMenu } from './menu-schema';

describe('parsePublicMenu', () => {
  it('pasa el JSON de la BD a camelCase y ordena alérgenos y etiquetas', () => {
    const data = parsePublicMenu(SAMPLE_MENU_JSON);
    expect(data?.unavailableMode).toBe('strike');
    const carta = data!.menus[0];
    expect(carta).toMatchObject({ id: 'carta-1', kind: 'carta', priceCents: null, weekdays: null, startMinute: null, dateFrom: null });
    const first = carta.sections[0].items[0];
    expect(first.kind).toBe('dish');
    if (first.kind === 'dish') {
      expect(first.dish).toMatchObject({
        id: 'd-croquetas',

        allergens: ['gluten', 'milk'],
        tags: ['recommended'],
      });
      expect(first.dish.prices[0]).toEqual({ kind: 'fixed', priceCents: 900, labels: { es: 'Media', en: 'Half' } });
    }
    expect(carta.sections[0].items[1].kind === 'dish' && carta.sections[0].items[1].dish.tags).toEqual(['vegan', 'gluten_free']);
    const daily = data!.menus[1];

    expect(parsePublicMenu(undefined)).toBeNull();

});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { getMessages } from '@/lib/i18n/messages';
import { SAMPLE_MENU_JSON } from './menu-fixtures';
import { parsePublicMenu, type PublicMenuData } from './menu-schema';
import { buildMenuView, menusToShow } from './menu-view';

const data = parsePublicMenu(SAMPLE_MENU_JSON) as PublicMenuData;
const [carta, daily] = data.menus;
const mediaUrl = (p: string) => `https://cdn.test/${p}`;
const nb = (s: string) => s.replace(/ /g, ' ');
const view = (menu = carta, locale: 'es' | 'en' = 'es', unavailableMode: 'hide' | 'strike' = 'strike') =>
  buildMenuView(menu, { locale, unavailableMode, messages: getMessages(locale), mediaUrl });

describe('buildMenuView', () => {
  it('textos en el idioma pedido, con el castellano de respaldo', () => {
    const v = view(carta, 'en');
    expect(v.title).toBe('Menu');
    expect(v.sections.map((s) => s.title)).toEqual(['Starters', 'Principales']);
    expect(v.sections[0].items[0]).toMatchObject({ name: 'Croquettes', description: 'De jamón ibérico.' });
  });

  it('precios con formato y etiqueta', () => {
    const v = view();
    expect(v.sections[0].items[0].prices).toEqual([
      { label: 'Media', text: nb('9,00 €'), market: false },
      { label: 'Ración', text: nb('15,00 €'), market: false },
    ]);
    expect(v.sections[1].items.map((i) => i.prices)).toEqual([
      [{ label: null, text: nb('45,00 €/kg'), market: false }],
      [{ label: null, text: 'S/M', market: true }],
    ]);
    expect(view(carta, 'en').sections[0].items[0].prices.map((p) => [p.label, p.text])).toEqual([
      ['Half', '€9.00'],
      ['Ración', '€15.00'],
    ]);
  });

  it('agotados: tachados con «strike» y fuera con «hide»', () => {
    expect(view().sections[0].items.map((i) => [i.name, i.unavailable])).toEqual([
      ['Croquetas', false],
      ['Ensalada de la huerta', true],
    ]);
    expect(view(carta, 'es', 'hide').sections[0].items.map((i) => i.name)).toEqual(['Croquetas']);
  });

  it('menú del día: precio cerrado, «incluye» y platos escritos a mano con sus alérgenos', () => {
    const v = view(daily);
    expect([v.kind, v.title, v.priceText, v.includes]).toEqual(['daily', 'Menú del día', nb('14,50 €'), 'Pan, bebida y postre']);
    expect(v.sections[0].items[0]).toEqual({
      id: 'i-sopa',
      name: 'Sopa de pescado',
      description: null,
      prices: [],
      allergens: ['fish', 'celery'],
      tags: [],
      photoUrl: null,
      photoAlt: 'Foto de Sopa de pescado',
      enlargeLabel: 'Ampliar la foto de Sopa de pescado',
      unavailable: false,
    });
  });

  it('las secciones vacías no salen; las fotos llevan su URL y sus textos alternativos', () => {
    const v = view();
    expect(v.sections.map((s) => s.id)).toEqual(['entrantes', 'principales']);
    expect(v.sections[0].items[0]).toMatchObject({
      photoUrl: 'https://cdn.test/11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111/dishes/croquetas.webp',
      photoAlt: 'Foto de Croquetas',
      enlargeLabel: 'Ampliar la foto de Croquetas',
      allergens: ['gluten', 'milk'],
      tags: ['recommended'],
    });
    expect(v.priceText).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('menusToShow', () => {
  it('el menú del día que toca va primero y las cartas sin platos no salen', () => {
    expect(menusToShow(data, new Date('2026-11-30T12:30:00Z'), 'Europe/Madrid').map((m) => m.id)).toEqual(['daily-1', 'carta-1']);
    expect(menusToShow(data, new Date('2026-12-05T12:30:00Z'), 'Europe/Madrid').map((m) => m.id)).toEqual(['carta-1']);
    expect(menusToShow(null, new Date(), 'Europe/Madrid')).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Escribir el esquema**

Create `src/lib/public/menu-schema.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from 'zod';
import { MENU_KINDS, PRICE_KINDS, sortAllergens, sortTags, UNAVAILABLE_MODES } from '@/lib/menu/codes';
import { toI18nText } from '@/lib/menu/i18n-text';

const i18n = z.record(z.string(), z.unknown()).transform(toI18nText);
const minutes = z.number().int().nullable();

const price = z
  .object({ kind: z.enum(PRICE_KINDS), price_cents: z.number().int().nullable(), labels: i18n })
  .transform((p) => ({ kind: p.kind, priceCents: p.price_cents, labels: p.labels }));

const dish = z
  .object({
    id: z.string(),
    names: i18n,
    descriptions: i18n,
    photo_path: z.string().nullable(),
    allergens: z.array(z.string()),
    tags: z.array(z.string()),
    unavailable: z.boolean(),
    prices: z.array(price),
  })
  .transform((d) => ({
    id: d.id,
    names: d.names,
    descriptions: d.descriptions,
    photoPath: d.photo_path,
    allergens: sortAllergens(d.allergens),
    tags: sortTags(d.tags),
    unavailable: d.unavailable,
    prices: d.prices,
  }));

const item = z
  .object({ id: z.string(), inline_names: i18n.nullable(), inline_allergens: z.array(z.string()), dish: dish.nullable() })
  .transform((i) =>
    i.dish
      ? { id: i.id, kind: 'dish' as const, dish: i.dish }
      : { id: i.id, kind: 'inline' as const, names: i.inline_names ?? {}, allergens: sortAllergens(i.inline_allergens) },
  );

const section = z.object({ id: z.string(), names: i18n, items: z.array(item) });

const menu = z
  .object({
    id: z.string(),
    kind: z.enum(MENU_KINDS),
    names: i18n,
    price_cents: z.number().int().nullable(),
    includes: i18n,
    weekdays: z.array(z.number().int()).nullable(),
    start_minute: minutes,
    end_minute: minutes,
    date_from: z.string().nullable(),
    date_to: z.string().nullable(),
    sections: z.array(section),
  })
  .transform((m) => ({
    id: m.id,
    kind: m.kind,
    names: m.names,
    priceCents: m.price_cents,
    includes: m.includes,
    weekdays: m.weekdays,
    startMinute: m.start_minute,
    endMinute: m.end_minute,
    dateFrom: m.date_from,
    dateTo: m.date_to,
    sections: m.sections,
  }));

/** JSON de public.get_public_menu() (snake_case) → objeto de la app (camelCase). */
const publicMenuSchema = z
  .object({ unavailable_mode: z.enum(UNAVAILABLE_MODES), menus: z.array(menu) })
  .transform((d) => ({ unavailableMode: d.unavailable_mode, menus: d.menus }));

export type PublicMenuData = z.output<typeof publicMenuSchema>;
export type PublicMenu = PublicMenuData['menus'][number];
export type PublicSection = PublicMenu['sections'][number];
export type PublicItem = PublicSection['items'][number];
export type PublicDish = Extract<PublicItem, { kind: 'dish' }>['dish'];

/** null si el restaurante no está activo (o no existe). Lanza si la forma no es la esperada. */
export function parsePublicMenu(data: unknown): PublicMenuData | null {
  if (data === null || data === undefined) return null;
  return publicMenuSchema.parse(data);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Escribir la vista**

Create `src/lib/public/menu-view.ts`:

```ts
import type { Locale } from '@/lib/i18n/locales';
import type { PublicMessages } from '@/lib/i18n/messages';
import type { Allergen, DishTag, MenuKind, UnavailableMode } from '@/lib/menu/codes';
import { textFor } from '@/lib/menu/i18n-text';
import { formatCents, formatDishPrice } from '@/lib/menu/price';
import { visibleMenus } from '@/lib/menu/schedule';
import type { PublicMenu, PublicMenuData } from './menu-schema';

export type MenuViewPrice = { label: string | null; text: string; market: boolean };
export type MenuViewItem = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description: string | null;
  prices: MenuViewPrice[];
  allergens: Allergen[];
  tags: DishTag[];
  photoUrl: string | null;
  photoAlt: string;
  enlargeLabel: string;
  unavailable: boolean;
};
export type MenuViewSection = { id: string; title: string; items: MenuViewItem[] };
/** Una carta lista para pintar. Todo es serializable: la recibe el componente cliente de la carta. */
export type MenuView = {
  id: string;
  kind: MenuKind;
  title: string;
  priceText: string | null;
  includes: string | null;
  sections: MenuViewSection[];
};

/**
 * Textos en el idioma (o en castellano si falta), precios con formato y agotados ocultos o marcados según el
 * ajuste del restaurante. Las secciones que se quedan sin platos no salen.
 */
export function buildMenuView(
  menu: PublicMenu,
  opts: { locale: Locale; unavailableMode: UnavailableMode; messages: PublicMessages; mediaUrl: (path: string) => string },
): MenuView {
  const { locale, messages: m } = opts;
  const units = { perKg: m.perKg, per100g: m.per100g, market: m.marketPrice };
  const sections = menu.sections
    .map((s) => ({
      id: s.id,
      title: textFor(s.names, locale) ?? '',
      items: s.items.flatMap((item): MenuViewItem[] => {
        if (item.kind === 'inline') {
          const name = textFor(item.names, locale) ?? '';
          return [
            {
              id: item.id,
              name,
              description: null,
              prices: [],
              allergens: item.allergens,
              tags: [],
              photoUrl: null,
              photoAlt: m.dishPhotoAlt(name),
              enlargeLabel: m.enlargePhoto(name),
              unavailable: false,
            },
          ];
        }
        const d = item.dish;
        if (d.unavailable && opts.unavailableMode === 'hide') return [];
        const name = textFor(d.names, locale) ?? '';
        return [
          {
            id: item.id,
            name,
            description: textFor(d.descriptions, locale),
            prices: d.prices.map((p) => ({ label: textFor(p.labels, locale), text: formatDishPrice(p, locale, units), market: p.kind === 'market' })),
            allergens: d.allergens,
            tags: d.tags,
            photoUrl: d.photoPath ? opts.mediaUrl(d.photoPath) : null,
            photoAlt: m.dishPhotoAlt(name),
            enlargeLabel: m.enlargePhoto(name),
            unavailable: d.unavailable,
          },
        ];
      }),
    }))
    .filter((s) => s.items.length > 0);
  return {
    id: menu.id,
    kind: menu.kind,
    title: textFor(menu.names, locale) ?? '',
    priceText: menu.priceCents === null ? null : formatCents(menu.priceCents, locale),
    includes: textFor(menu.includes, locale),
    sections,
  };
}

/** Cartas que se ven ahora y tienen algún plato, en el orden de las pestañas (menús del día y especiales primero). */
export function menusToShow(data: PublicMenuData | null, now: Date, timeZone: string): PublicMenu[] {
  if (!data) return [];
  return visibleMenus(data.menus, now, timeZone).filter((m) => m.sections.some((s) => s.items.length > 0));
}
```

```ts
/** Dirección de la carta pública. */
export function menuPath(slug: string): string {
  return `/${slug}/menu`;
}

/** Pestaña de una carta, conservando el idioma: /la-taberna/menu?lang=en&carta=<id>. */
export function menuTabHref(slug: string, locale: Locale, menuId: string): string {
  return `${localeHref(menuPath(slug), locale)}&carta=${encodeURIComponent(menuId)}`;
}
```

In `src/lib/public/jsonld.ts`, change the signature and add `hasMenu`:

```ts
export function restaurantJsonLd(
  r: PublicRestaurant,
  opts: { url: string; mediaUrl: (path: string) => string; menuUrl?: string },
): Record<string, unknown> {
  const ld: Record<string, unknown> = { '@context': 'https://schema.org', '@type': 'Restaurant', name: r.name, url: opts.url };
  if (opts.menuUrl) ld.hasMenu = opts.menuUrl;
```

(the rest of the function stays the same).

In `src/lib/public/data.ts`, add the import:

```ts
import { parsePublicMenu, type PublicMenuData } from './menu-schema';
```

and add at the end of the file:

```ts
/**
 * Carta pública por dirección (solo restaurantes activos). Misma etiqueta que la mini-web: las acciones del menú
 * invalidan las dos. La visibilidad del menú del día no se cachea: la calcula la página al pintar.
 */
export const getPublicMenu = cache(async (rawSlug: string): Promise<PublicMenuData | null> => {
  const slug = rawSlug.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!isValidSlug(slug)) return null;
  return unstable_cache(
    async () => {
      const { data, error } = await createPublicClient().rpc('get_public_menu', { p_slug: slug });
      if (error) throw new Error(`get_public_menu: ${error.message}`);
      return parsePublicMenu(data);
    },
    ['public-menu', CACHE_VERSION, slug],
    { tags: [publicRestaurantTag(slug), PUBLIC_ALL_TAG], revalidate: REVALIDATE_SECONDS },
  )();
});

/** Para sitemap.xml: restaurantes activos con alguna carta con platos. */
export const getPublicMenuSlugs = unstable_cache(
  async (): Promise<PublicSlug[]> => {
    const { data, error } = await createPublicClient().rpc('list_public_menus');
    if (error) throw new Error(`list_public_menus: ${error.message}`);
    return ((data ?? []) as { slug: string; enabled_locales: string[]; updated_at: string }[]).map((r) => ({
      slug: r.slug,
      enabledLocales: sortLocales(r.enabled_locales),
      updatedAt: r.updated_at,
    }));
  },
  ['public-menu-slugs', CACHE_VERSION],
  { tags: [PUBLIC_ALL_TAG], revalidate: REVALIDATE_SECONDS },
);
```

**Files:**

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `QR_CARD_TEXT` (Task 7), `BRAND`.
- Produces:
  - `qr.ts`: `QR_PNG_SIZE = 1024`, `QR_QUIET_ZONE = 4`; `menuQrUrl(siteUrl, slug): string`; `qrMatrix(text): boolean[][]`; `qrRuns(matrix): { row; col; length }[]`; `qrPng(text): Promise<Buffer>`.
  - `qr-layout.ts` (puro): `A4 = { width: 595.28, height: 841.89 }`; `CARD_MARGIN = 22`, `MARK_LENGTH = 24`, `STRIPE_HEIGHT = 8`, `LOGO_BOX = 64`, `QR_BOX = 190`, `NAME_SIZE`, `CAPTION_SIZE`, `URL_SIZE`; `type Rect`, `type Segment`, `type CardLayout = { stripe; logo: Rect | null; centerX; textWidth; nameBaseline; qr; captionBaseline; urlBaseline }`; `cardRects(page = A4): Rect[]` (arriba izquierda, arriba derecha, abajo izquierda, abajo derecha; origen abajo a la izquierda, como en PDF); `cropMarks(page = A4, length = MARK_LENGTH): Segment[]`; `cardLayout(card, hasLogo): CardLayout`; `fitText(text, measure, maxWidth, maxSize, minSize): { text; size }`; `toWinAnsi(text): string`.
  - `qr-pdf.ts`: `type QrCardsInput = { name; url; accentColor; logoPng: Uint8Array | null }`; `buildQrCardsPdf(input): Promise<Uint8Array>`.
  - `logo-png.ts`: `LOGO_PNG_MAX = 512`; `logoToPng(bytes: Uint8Array): Promise<Uint8Array>`; `fetchLogoPng(url): Promise<Uint8Array | null>` (null si no se puede: el PDF sale sin logo). Usa `sharp`: solo en el servidor (Node.js).

Lee antes: `node_modules/qrcode/README.md` (`create`, `toBuffer`), `node_modules/pdf-lib/README.md` (`PDFDocument.create`, `addPage`, `embedFont`, `embedPng`, `drawRectangle`, `drawText`, `drawLine`, `save`) y `node_modules/sharp/lib/index.d.ts` (`resize`, `png`, `toBuffer`, `metadata`) después de instalarlas.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { menuQrUrl, QR_PNG_SIZE, qrMatrix, qrPng, qrRuns } from './qr';

describe('QR de la carta', () => {
  it('apunta a la carta con la dirección absoluta y sin idioma', () => {
    expect(menuQrUrl('https://mesa.example.com/', 'la-taberna')).toBe('https://mesa.example.com/la-taberna/menu');
    expect(menuQrUrl('https://mesa.example.com', 'la-taberna')).toBe('https://mesa.example.com/la-taberna/menu');
  });

  it('la matriz es cuadrada y tiene los tres cuadros de posición', () => {
    const m = qrMatrix('https://mesa.example.com/la-taberna/menu');
    const n = m.length;
    expect(n).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(21);
    expect(m.every((row) => row.length === n)).toBe(true);
    for (const [r, c] of [
      [0, 0],
      [0, n - 7],
      [n - 7, 0],
    ]) {
      expect(m[r].slice(c, c + 7).every(Boolean)).toBe(true);
      expect(m[r + 6].slice(c, c + 7).every(Boolean)).toBe(true);
      expect(m[r + 1][c + 1]).toBe(false);
      expect(m[r + 3][c + 3]).toBe(true);
    }
  });

  it('qrRuns junta los módulos oscuros seguidos de cada fila', () => {
    expect(
      qrRuns([
        [true, true, false, true],
        [false, false, false, false],
        [false, true, true, true],
      ]),
    ).toEqual([
      { row: 0, col: 0, length: 2 },
      { row: 0, col: 3, length: 1 },
      { row: 2, col: 1, length: 3 },
    ]);
    const m = qrMatrix('https://mesa.example.com/x/menu');
    expect(qrRuns(m).reduce((sum, run) => sum + run.length, 0)).toBe(m.flat().filter(Boolean).length);
  });

  it('el PNG mide 1024 × 1024', async () => {
    const png = await qrPng('https://mesa.example.com/la-taberna/menu');
    expect(png.subarray(0, 8).toString('hex')).toBe('89504e470d0a1a0a');
    expect([png.readUInt32BE(16), png.readUInt32BE(20)]).toEqual([QR_PNG_SIZE, QR_PNG_SIZE]);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { A4, CARD_MARGIN, cardLayout, cardRects, cropMarks, fitText, toWinAnsi, type Rect } from './qr-layout';

const mm = (pt: number) => (pt / 72) * 25.4;

describe('hoja A4 con 4 tarjetas A6', () => {
  it('cuatro tarjetas iguales de 105 × 148 mm que cubren el A4 sin solaparse', () => {
    const cards = cardRects();
    expect(cards).toHaveLength(4);
    for (const c of cards) {
      expect(c.width).toBeCloseTo(297.64, 2);
      expect(c.height).toBeCloseTo(420.945, 2);
    }
    expect(mm(cards[0].width)).toBeCloseTo(105, 0);
    expect(mm(cards[0].height)).toBeGreaterThan(148);
    expect(mm(cards[0].height)).toBeLessThan(148.6);
    expect(cards.reduce((sum, c) => sum + c.width * c.height, 0)).toBeCloseTo(A4.width * A4.height, 4);
    const overlap = (a: Rect, b: Rect) =>
      Math.max(0, Math.min(a.x + a.width, b.x + b.width) - Math.max(a.x, b.x)) *
      Math.max(0, Math.min(a.y + a.height, b.y + b.height) - Math.max(a.y, b.y));
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i += 1) for (let j = i + 1; j < 4; j += 1) expect(overlap(cards[i], cards[j])).toBeCloseTo(0, 6);
  });

  it('marcas de corte en las dos líneas centrales, dentro de la hoja', () => {
    const marks = cropMarks();
    expect(marks).toHaveLength(6);
    for (const s of marks) {
      for (const [x, y] of [
        [s.x1, s.y1],
        [s.x2, s.y2],
      ]) {
        expect(x >= 0 && x <= A4.width && y >= 0 && y <= A4.height).toBe(true);
      }
      if (s.x1 === s.x2) expect(s.x1).toBeCloseTo(A4.width / 2, 6);
      else expect(s.y1).toBeCloseTo(A4.height / 2, 6);
    }
    expect(marks.filter((s) => s.x1 === s.x2)).toHaveLength(3);
  });

  it('el contenido de cada tarjeta queda dentro de su margen y en orden, con y sin logo', () => {
    for (const card of cardRects()) {
      for (const hasLogo of [true, false]) {
        const l = cardLayout(card, hasLogo);
        const inside = (r: Rect) =>
          r.x >= card.x + CARD_MARGIN - 0.01 &&
          r.y >= card.y + CARD_MARGIN - 0.01 &&
          r.x + r.width <= card.x + card.width - CARD_MARGIN + 0.01 &&
          r.y + r.height <= card.y + card.height - CARD_MARGIN + 0.01;
        for (const r of [l.stripe, l.qr, ...(l.logo ? [l.logo] : [])]) expect(inside(r)).toBe(true);
        expect(l.logo === null).toBe(!hasLogo);
        expect(l.nameBaseline).toBeLessThan(l.logo ? l.logo.y : l.stripe.y);
        expect(l.qr.y + l.qr.height).toBeLessThan(l.nameBaseline);
        expect(l.captionBaseline).toBeLessThan(l.qr.y);
        expect(l.urlBaseline).toBeLessThan(l.captionBaseline);
        expect(l.urlBaseline - 2).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(card.y + CARD_MARGIN);
        expect(l.centerX).toBeCloseTo(card.x + card.width / 2, 6);
        expect(l.textWidth).toBeCloseTo(card.width - 2 * CARD_MARGIN, 6);
      }
    }
  });

  it('fitText reduce la letra hasta el mínimo y, si aun así no cabe, recorta con «…»', () => {
    const measure = (text: string, size: number) => text.length * size * 0.5;
    expect(fitText('La Taberna', measure, 200, 20, 11)).toEqual({ text: 'La Taberna', size: 20 });
    expect(fitText('x'.repeat(30), measure, 200, 20, 11)).toEqual({ text: 'x'.repeat(30), size: 13 });
    const cut = fitText('x'.repeat(60), measure, 200, 20, 11);
    expect(cut).toEqual({ text: `${'x'.repeat(35)}…`, size: 11 });
  });

  it('toWinAnsi deja solo lo que saben escribir las fuentes estándar del PDF', () => {
    expect(toWinAnsi('Café · Ñandú € «Œuf»')).toBe('Café · Ñandú € «Œuf»');
    expect(toWinAnsi('Crème brûlée')).toBe('Crème brûlée');
    expect(toWinAnsi('Łódź  🍕 Bar')).toBe('ódz Bar');
  });
});
```

```ts
import { PDFDocument } from 'pdf-lib';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { qrPng } from './qr';
import { A4 } from './qr-layout';
import { buildQrCardsPdf } from './qr-pdf';

describe('PDF de tarjetas QR', () => {
  it('una sola hoja A4, con logo', async () => {
    const logoPng = new Uint8Array(await qrPng('logo de prueba'));
    const bytes = await buildQrCardsPdf({
      name: 'La Taberna de Pepe',
      url: 'https://mesa.example.com/la-taberna/menu',

    const bytes = await buildQrCardsPdf({
      name: 'Łódź 🍕 Bar con un nombre larguísimo que no cabe de ninguna manera en una tarjeta A6',
      url: 'https://mesa.example.com/lodz/menu',
      accentColor: '#FFC48A',
      logoPng: null,

});
```

```ts
import sharp from 'sharp';

import { LOGO_PNG_MAX, logoToPng } from './logo-png';

describe('logo para el PDF', () => {
  it('pasa WebP y JPEG a PNG, sin agrandar los pequeños y reduciendo los grandes', async () => {
    const small = await sharp({ create: { width: 40, height: 20, channels: 4, background: '#FF7A1A' } }).webp().toBuffer();

    expect(await sharp(await logoToPng(new Uint8Array(big))).metadata()).toMatchObject({ width: LOGO_PNG_MAX, height: 256 });
  });
});
```

```ts
import { create, toBuffer } from 'qrcode';

/** Lado del PNG del QR, en píxeles. */
export const QR_PNG_SIZE = 1024;
/** Margen blanco alrededor del QR, en módulos (lo que pide el estándar para que se lea bien). */
export const QR_QUIET_ZONE = 4;

/** Dirección que lleva el QR: absoluta y sin ?lang= (cada móvil elige su idioma). */
export function menuQrUrl(siteUrl: string, slug: string): string {
  return `${siteUrl.replace(/\/+$/, '')}/${slug}/menu`;
}

/** Módulos del QR (true = oscuro), con corrección de errores M (se sigue leyendo con un 15 % dañado). */
export function qrMatrix(text: string): boolean[][] {
  const { modules } = create(text, { errorCorrectionLevel: 'M' });
  return Array.from({ length: modules.size }, (_, row) =>
    Array.from({ length: modules.size }, (_, col) => modules.get(row, col) === 1),
  );
}

/** Tramos horizontales de módulos oscuros: menos rectángulos en el PDF y sin rayas finas entre módulos. */
export function qrRuns(matrix: boolean[][]): { row: number; col: number; length: number }[] {
  const runs: { row: number; col: number; length: number }[] = [];
  matrix.forEach((cells, row) => {
    let start = -1;
    cells.forEach((dark, col) => {
      if (dark && start < 0) start = col;
      const last = col === cells.length - 1;
      if (start >= 0 && (!dark || last)) {
        const end = dark && last ? col + 1 : col;
        runs.push({ row, col: start, length: end - start });
        start = -1;
      }
    });
  });
  return runs;
}

/** PNG de 1024 px con 4 módulos de margen: tinta oscura de FByte sobre blanco. */
export function qrPng(text: string): Promise<Buffer> {
  return toBuffer(text, {
    type: 'png',
    width: QR_PNG_SIZE,
    margin: QR_QUIET_ZONE,
    errorCorrectionLevel: 'M',
    color: { dark: '#0A1330FF', light: '#FFFFFFFF' },
  });
}
```

```ts
/** A4 en puntos PDF (1 pt = 1/72 de pulgada). Cada tarjeta A6 es un cuarto exacto. */
export const A4 = { width: 595.28, height: 841.89 } as const;
/** Margen de seguridad dentro de cada tarjeta (las impresoras no llegan al borde de la hoja). */
export const CARD_MARGIN = 22;
export const MARK_LENGTH = 24;
export const STRIPE_HEIGHT = 8;
export const LOGO_BOX = 64;
/** Caja del QR, con sus 4 módulos de margen blanco incluidos. */
export const QR_BOX = 190;
export const NAME_SIZE = { max: 20, min: 11 } as const;
export const CAPTION_SIZE = { max: 9, min: 6 } as const;
export const URL_SIZE = { max: 8, min: 5 } as const;

/** Coordenadas PDF: origen abajo a la izquierda. */
export type Rect = { x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number };
export type Segment = { x1: number; y1: number; x2: number; y2: number };
export type CardLayout = {
  /** Franja con el color de acento, arriba. */
  stripe: Rect;
  /** Caja del logo (el logo se encaja dentro sin deformarse). */
  logo: Rect | null;
  centerX: number;
  /** Ancho máximo de los textos. */
  textWidth: number;
  nameBaseline: number;
  qr: Rect;
  captionBaseline: number;
  urlBaseline: number;
};

/** Las 4 tarjetas: arriba a la izquierda, arriba a la derecha, abajo a la izquierda y abajo a la derecha. */
export function cardRects(page: { width: number; height: number } = A4): Rect[] {
  const width = page.width / 2;
  const height = page.height / 2;
  return [
    { x: 0, y: height, width, height },
    { x: width, y: height, width, height },
    { x: 0, y: 0, width, height },
    { x: width, y: 0, width, height },
  ];
}

/** Marcas de corte en las dos líneas centrales: en los cuatro bordes de la hoja y una cruz en el centro. */
export function cropMarks(page: { width: number; height: number } = A4, length = MARK_LENGTH): Segment[] {
  const cx = page.width / 2;
  const cy = page.height / 2;
  return [
    { x1: cx, y1: page.height, x2: cx, y2: page.height - length },
    { x1: cx, y1: 0, x2: cx, y2: length },
    { x1: 0, y1: cy, x2: length, y2: cy },
    { x1: page.width, y1: cy, x2: page.width - length, y2: cy },
    { x1: cx - length / 2, y1: cy, x2: cx + length / 2, y2: cy },
    { x1: cx, y1: cy - length / 2, x2: cx, y2: cy + length / 2 },
  ];
}

/** De arriba abajo: franja de acento, logo (si hay), nombre, QR, texto fijo y dirección web. */
export function cardLayout(card: Rect, hasLogo: boolean): CardLayout {
  const centerX = card.x + card.width / 2;
  const top = card.y + card.height - CARD_MARGIN;
  const stripe = { x: card.x + CARD_MARGIN, y: top - STRIPE_HEIGHT, width: card.width - 2 * CARD_MARGIN, height: STRIPE_HEIGHT };
  let cursor = stripe.y - 16;
  let logo: Rect | null = null;
  if (hasLogo) {
    logo = { x: centerX - LOGO_BOX / 2, y: cursor - LOGO_BOX, width: LOGO_BOX, height: LOGO_BOX };
    cursor = logo.y - 10;
  }
  const nameBaseline = cursor - NAME_SIZE.max;
  const qr = { x: centerX - QR_BOX / 2, y: nameBaseline - 8 - QR_BOX, width: QR_BOX, height: QR_BOX };
  const captionBaseline = qr.y - 8 - CAPTION_SIZE.max;
  const urlBaseline = captionBaseline - 6 - URL_SIZE.max;
  return { stripe, logo, centerX, textWidth: card.width - 2 * CARD_MARGIN, nameBaseline, qr, captionBaseline, urlBaseline };
}

/** Tamaño de letra que cabe (de maxSize a minSize, de medio en medio punto); si ni así cabe, recorta con «…». */
export function fitText(
  text: string,
  measure: (text: string, size: number) => number,
  maxWidth: number,
  maxSize: number,
  minSize: number,
): { text: string; size: number } {
  for (let size = maxSize; size >= minSize; size -= 0.5) {
    if (measure(text, size) <= maxWidth) return { text, size };
  }
  let cut = text;
  while (cut.length > 1 && measure(`${cut}…`, minSize) > maxWidth) cut = cut.slice(0, -1);
  return { text: `${cut.trimEnd()}…`, size: minSize };
}

/** Caracteres de WinAnsi (Windows-1252) fuera de Latin-1. */
const WIN_ANSI_EXTRA = '€‚ƒ„…†‡ˆ‰Š‹ŒŽ‘’“”•–—˜™š›œžŸ';

function isWinAnsi(ch: string): boolean {
  const code = ch.codePointAt(0) ?? 0;
  return (code >= 0x20 && code <= 0x7e) || (code >= 0xa0 && code <= 0xff) || WIN_ANSI_EXTRA.includes(ch);
}

/**
 * Las fuentes estándar del PDF (Helvetica) solo saben WinAnsi: «ź» pasa a «z» y lo que no tiene arreglo
 * (por ejemplo, «Ł» o los emojis) se quita.
 */
export function toWinAnsi(text: string): string {
  let out = '';
  for (const ch of text.normalize('NFC')) {
    if (isWinAnsi(ch)) {
      out += ch;
      continue;
    }
    const base = ch.normalize('NFD').replace(/[̀-ͯ]/g, '');
    if (base && [...base].every(isWinAnsi)) out += base;
  }
  return out.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
}
```

Create `src/lib/menu/qr-pdf.ts`:

```ts
import { PDFDocument, rgb, StandardFonts, type PDFFont, type PDFPage, type RGB } from 'pdf-lib';
import { BRAND } from '@/config/brand';
import { QR_CARD_TEXT } from '@/lib/i18n/messages';
import { QR_QUIET_ZONE, qrMatrix, qrRuns } from './qr';
import { A4, CAPTION_SIZE, cardLayout, cardRects, cropMarks, fitText, NAME_SIZE, toWinAnsi, URL_SIZE } from './qr-layout';

export type QrCardsInput = { name: string; url: string; accentColor: string; logoPng: Uint8Array | null };

const INK = rgb(10 / 255, 19 / 255, 48 / 255);
const MARK = rgb(0.45, 0.45, 0.45);

function hexColor(hex: string): RGB {
  const n = Number.parseInt(hex.replace('#', ''), 16);
  return rgb(((n >> 16) & 255) / 255, ((n >> 8) & 255) / 255, (n & 255) / 255);
}

function drawCentered(page: PDFPage, text: string, font: PDFFont, size: number, centerX: number, baseline: number) {
  page.drawText(text, { x: centerX - font.widthOfTextAtSize(text, size) / 2, y: baseline, size, font, color: INK });
}

/** Hoja A4 con 4 tarjetas A6 iguales (franja de acento, logo, nombre, QR, texto fijo y dirección) y marcas de corte. */
export async function buildQrCardsPdf(input: QrCardsInput): Promise<Uint8Array> {
  const doc = await PDFDocument.create();
  doc.setTitle(`Código QR de la carta · ${input.name}`);
  doc.setCreator(`${BRAND.productName} · ${BRAND.company}`);
  doc.setProducer(BRAND.productName);
  const page = doc.addPage([A4.width, A4.height]);
  const bold = await doc.embedFont(StandardFonts.HelveticaBold);
  const regular = await doc.embedFont(StandardFonts.Helvetica);
  const logo = input.logoPng ? await doc.embedPng(input.logoPng) : null;
  const matrix = qrMatrix(input.url);
  const runs = qrRuns(matrix);
  const accent = hexColor(input.accentColor);
  const name = toWinAnsi(input.name) || 'Carta';
  const caption = toWinAnsi(QR_CARD_TEXT);
  const address = toWinAnsi(input.url.replace(/^https?:\/\//, ''));

  for (const card of cardRects()) {
    const l = cardLayout(card, logo !== null);
    page.drawRectangle({ ...l.stripe, color: accent });
    if (logo && l.logo) {
      const scale = Math.min(l.logo.width / logo.width, l.logo.height / logo.height);
      const width = logo.width * scale;
      const height = logo.height * scale;
      page.drawImage(logo, { x: l.logo.x + (l.logo.width - width) / 2, y: l.logo.y + (l.logo.height - height) / 2, width, height });
    }
    const fittedName = fitText(name, (t, s) => bold.widthOfTextAtSize(t, s), l.textWidth, NAME_SIZE.max, NAME_SIZE.min);
    drawCentered(page, fittedName.text, bold, fittedName.size, l.centerX, l.nameBaseline);
    // El QR en vectores (nítido a cualquier tamaño), con 4 módulos de margen blanco alrededor.
    const cell = l.qr.width / (matrix.length + 2 * QR_QUIET_ZONE);
    for (const run of runs) {
      page.drawRectangle({
        x: l.qr.x + (QR_QUIET_ZONE + run.col) * cell,
        y: l.qr.y + l.qr.height - (QR_QUIET_ZONE + run.row + 1) * cell,
        width: run.length * cell,
        height: cell,
        color: INK,
      });
    }
    const fittedCaption = fitText(caption, (t, s) => regular.widthOfTextAtSize(t, s), l.textWidth, CAPTION_SIZE.max, CAPTION_SIZE.min);
    drawCentered(page, fittedCaption.text, regular, fittedCaption.size, l.centerX, l.captionBaseline);
    const fittedUrl = fitText(address, (t, s) => bold.widthOfTextAtSize(t, s), l.textWidth, URL_SIZE.max, URL_SIZE.min);
    drawCentered(page, fittedUrl.text, bold, fittedUrl.size, l.centerX, l.urlBaseline);
  }
  for (const s of cropMarks()) {
    page.drawLine({ start: { x: s.x1, y: s.y1 }, end: { x: s.x2, y: s.y2 }, thickness: 0.5, color: MARK });
  }
  return doc.save();
}
```

Create `src/lib/menu/logo-png.ts`:

```ts
import sharp from 'sharp';

/** Lado mayor del logo que se incrusta en el PDF, en píxeles (de sobra para 64 pt impresos). */
export const LOGO_PNG_MAX = 512;

/** Logo del bucket (WebP o JPEG) → PNG, que es lo que sabe incrustar pdf-lib. Solo en el servidor (Node.js). */
export async function logoToPng(bytes: Uint8Array): Promise<Uint8Array> {
  const png = await sharp(bytes)
    .resize({ width: LOGO_PNG_MAX, height: LOGO_PNG_MAX, fit: 'inside', withoutEnlargement: true })
    .png()
    .toBuffer();
  return new Uint8Array(png);
}

/** Descarga el logo público y lo pasa a PNG. null si no se puede: el PDF sale sin logo. */
export async function fetchLogoPng(url: string): Promise<Uint8Array | null> {
  try {
    const res = await fetch(url, { cache: 'no-store' });
    if (!res.ok) return null;
    return await logoToPng(new Uint8Array(await res.arrayBuffer()));
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { dishesByName, dishesNotInMenu, menusWithDish, parseMenuTree, pricesSummaryEs, unavailableGroups } from './tree';

const dish = (id: string, name: string, extra: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => ({

  names: { es: name },

  photo_path: null,

});
const schedule = { price_cents: null, includes: {}, weekdays: null, start_minute: null, end_minute: null, date_from: null, date_to: null };

const TREE = {
  enabled_locales: ['en', 'es'],
  unavailable_mode: 'strike',
  timezone: 'Atlantic/Canary',
  menus: [
    {
      id: 'carta',

          position: 0,

            { id: 'i5', position: 1, dish_id: 'croquetas', inline_names: null, inline_allergens: [] },
          ],
        },
      ],
    },
    {
      id: 'vieja',
      kind: 'carta',
      names: { es: 'Carta de verano' },
      active: false,
      position: 2,
      ...schedule,
      sections: [{ id: 's4', names: { es: 'Fríos' }, position: 0, items: [{ id: 'i6', position: 0, dish_id: 'gazpacho', inline_names: null, inline_allergens: [] }] }],
    },
  ],
  dishes: [
    dish('ensalada', 'Ensalada', { tags: ['vegan', 'gluten_free'] }),
    dish('croquetas', 'Croquetas', {
      allergens: ['milk', 'gluten'],
      unavailable: true,
      prices: [
        { kind: 'fixed', price_cents: 900, labels: { es: 'Media' } },
        { kind: 'fixed', price_cents: 1500, labels: { es: 'Ración' } },
      ],
    }),
    dish('flan', 'Flan'),
    dish('gazpacho', 'Gazpacho'),
    dish('alioli', 'Alioli'),
  ],
};

describe('árbol del menú para el panel', () => {
  it('pasa el JSON de get_menu_tree a camelCase', () => {
    const t = parseMenuTree(TREE);
    expect([t.enabledLocales, t.unavailableMode, t.timezone]).toEqual([['es', 'en'], 'strike', 'Atlantic/Canary']);
    expect(t.menus[1]).toMatchObject({ id: 'daily', kind: 'daily', priceCents: 1450, weekdays: [1, 2, 3, 4, 5], startMinute: null, active: true });
    expect(t.menus[1].sections[0].items[0]).toEqual({ id: 'i4', position: 0, dishId: null, inlineNames: { es: 'Sopa' }, inlineAllergens: ['fish', 'celery'] });
    expect(t.dishes[1]).toMatchObject({ id: 'croquetas', allergens: ['gluten', 'milk'], unavailable: true, photoPath: null });
    expect(t.dishes[1].prices[0]).toEqual({ kind: 'fixed', priceCents: 900, labels: { es: 'Media' } });
  });

  it('platos por nombre, cartas donde está un plato y platos que se pueden añadir a una carta', () => {
    const t = parseMenuTree(TREE);
    expect(dishesByName(t).map((d) => d.id)).toEqual(['alioli', 'croquetas', 'ensalada', 'flan', 'gazpacho']);
    expect(menusWithDish(t, 'croquetas').map((m) => m.id)).toEqual(['carta', 'daily']);
    expect(menusWithDish(t, 'alioli')).toEqual([]);
    expect(dishesNotInMenu(t, 'carta').map((d) => d.id)).toEqual(['alioli', 'gazpacho']);
    expect(dishesNotInMenu(t, 'daily').map((d) => d.id)).toEqual(['alioli', 'ensalada', 'flan', 'gazpacho']);
  });

  it('agotados: los platos de cada carta activa, sin repetir, y al final los que no están en ninguna', () => {
    const groups = unavailableGroups(parseMenuTree(TREE));
    expect(groups.map((g) => [g.title, g.dishes.map((d) => d.id)])).toEqual([
      ['Carta', ['croquetas', 'ensalada', 'flan']],
      ['Menú del día', ['croquetas']],
      ['Platos que no están en ninguna carta activa', ['alioli', 'gazpacho']],
    ]);
  });

  it('pricesSummaryEs resume los precios para el panel', () => {
    const t = parseMenuTree(TREE);
    expect(pricesSummaryEs(t.dishes[1].prices)).toBe('9,00 € (Media) · 15,00 € (Ración)');
    expect(pricesSummaryEs([{ kind: 'market', priceCents: null, labels: {} }])).toBe('S/M');
    expect(pricesSummaryEs([{ kind: 'per_kg', priceCents: 4500, labels: {} }])).toBe('45,00 €/kg');
    expect(pricesSummaryEs([])).toBe('Sin precio');
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Escribir `tree.ts`**

Create `src/lib/menu/tree.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from 'zod';
import { sortLocales, type Locale } from '@/lib/i18n/locales';
import {
  MENU_KINDS,
  PRICE_KINDS,
  sortAllergens,
  sortTags,
  UNAVAILABLE_MODES,
  type Allergen,
  type DishTag,
  type PriceKind,
  type UnavailableMode,
} from './codes';
import { textFor, toI18nText, type I18nText } from './i18n-text';
import { formatDishPrice } from './price';
import type { MenuSchedule } from './schedule';

export type PanelDishPrice = { kind: PriceKind; priceCents: number | null; labels: I18nText };
export type PanelDish = {
  id: string;
  names: I18nText;
  descriptions: I18nText;
  photoPath: string | null;
  allergens: Allergen[];
  tags: DishTag[];
  unavailable: boolean;
  prices: PanelDishPrice[];
};
export type PanelItem = { id: string; position: number; dishId: string | null; inlineNames: I18nText | null; inlineAllergens: Allergen[] };
export type PanelSection = { id: string; names: I18nText; position: number; items: PanelItem[] };
export type PanelMenu = MenuSchedule & {
  id: string;
  names: I18nText;
  includes: I18nText;
  active: boolean;
  position: number;
  priceCents: number | null;
  sections: PanelSection[];
};
export type MenuTree = {
  enabledLocales: Locale[];
  unavailableMode: UnavailableMode;
  timezone: string;
  menus: PanelMenu[];
  dishes: PanelDish[];
};

const i18n = z.record(z.string(), z.unknown()).transform(toI18nText);
const int = z.number().int();

const treeSchema = z.object({
  enabled_locales: z.array(z.string()),
  unavailable_mode: z.enum(UNAVAILABLE_MODES),
  timezone: z.string(),
  menus: z.array(
    z.object({
      id: z.string(),
      kind: z.enum(MENU_KINDS),
      names: i18n,
      includes: i18n,
      active: z.boolean(),
      position: int,
      price_cents: int.nullable(),
      weekdays: z.array(int).nullable(),
      start_minute: int.nullable(),
      end_minute: int.nullable(),
      date_from: z.string().nullable(),
      date_to: z.string().nullable(),
      sections: z.array(
        z.object({
          id: z.string(),
          names: i18n,
          position: int,
          items: z.array(
            z.object({
              id: z.string(),
              position: int,
              dish_id: z.string().nullable(),
              inline_names: i18n.nullable(),
              inline_allergens: z.array(z.string()),
            }),
          ),
        }),
      ),
    }),
  ),
  dishes: z.array(
    z.object({
      id: z.string(),
      names: i18n,
      descriptions: i18n,
      photo_path: z.string().nullable(),
      allergens: z.array(z.string()),
      tags: z.array(z.string()),
      unavailable: z.boolean(),
      prices: z.array(z.object({ kind: z.enum(PRICE_KINDS), price_cents: int.nullable(), labels: i18n })),
    }),
  ),
});

/** JSON de public.get_menu_tree() → objeto de la app. Lanza si la forma no es la esperada. */
export function parseMenuTree(data: unknown): MenuTree {
  const t = treeSchema.parse(data);
  return {
    enabledLocales: sortLocales(t.enabled_locales),
    unavailableMode: t.unavailable_mode,
    timezone: t.timezone,
    menus: t.menus.map((m) => ({
      id: m.id,
      kind: m.kind,
      names: m.names,
      includes: m.includes,
      active: m.active,
      position: m.position,
      priceCents: m.price_cents,
      weekdays: m.weekdays,
      startMinute: m.start_minute,
      endMinute: m.end_minute,
      dateFrom: m.date_from,
      dateTo: m.date_to,
      sections: m.sections.map((s) => ({
        id: s.id,
        names: s.names,
        position: s.position,
        items: s.items.map((i) => ({
          id: i.id,
          position: i.position,
          dishId: i.dish_id,
          inlineNames: i.inline_names,
          inlineAllergens: sortAllergens(i.inline_allergens),
        })),
      })),
    })),
    dishes: t.dishes.map((d) => ({
      id: d.id,
      names: d.names,
      descriptions: d.descriptions,
      photoPath: d.photo_path,
      allergens: sortAllergens(d.allergens),
      tags: sortTags(d.tags),
      unavailable: d.unavailable,
      prices: d.prices.map((p) => ({ kind: p.kind, priceCents: p.price_cents, labels: p.labels })),
    })),
  };
}

/** Nombre en castellano (el panel va en castellano). */
export function nameEs(names: I18nText | null | undefined): string {
  return textFor(names, 'es') ?? '';
}

/** Platos por orden alfabético (sin distinguir acentos ni mayúsculas). */
export function dishesByName(tree: MenuTree): PanelDish[] {
  return [...tree.dishes].sort((a, b) => nameEs(a.names).localeCompare(nameEs(b.names), 'es', { sensitivity: 'base' }));
}

/** Cartas en las que aparece un plato. */
export function menusWithDish(tree: MenuTree, dishId: string): PanelMenu[] {
  return tree.menus.filter((m) => m.sections.some((s) => s.items.some((i) => i.dishId === dishId)));
}

/** Platos que se pueden añadir a una carta: los que todavía no están en ella. */
export function dishesNotInMenu(tree: MenuTree, menuId: string): PanelDish[] {
  const menu = tree.menus.find((m) => m.id === menuId);
  const used = new Set(menu?.sections.flatMap((s) => s.items.map((i) => i.dishId)) ?? []);
  return dishesByName(tree).filter((d) => !used.has(d.id));
}

/** Pantalla de agotados: los platos de cada carta activa (sin repetir dentro de una carta) y, al final, el resto. */
export function unavailableGroups(tree: MenuTree): { id: string; title: string; dishes: PanelDish[] }[] {
  const byId = new Map(tree.dishes.map((d) => [d.id, d]));
  const placed = new Set<string>();
  const groups: { id: string; title: string; dishes: PanelDish[] }[] = [];
  for (const menu of tree.menus.filter((m) => m.active)) {
    const seen = new Set<string>();
    const dishes: PanelDish[] = [];
    for (const section of menu.sections) {
      for (const item of section.items) {
        const dish = item.dishId ? byId.get(item.dishId) : undefined;
        if (dish && !seen.has(dish.id)) {
          seen.add(dish.id);
          placed.add(dish.id);
          dishes.push(dish);
        }
      }
    }
    if (dishes.length > 0) groups.push({ id: menu.id, title: nameEs(menu.names), dishes });
  }
  const others = dishesByName(tree).filter((d) => !placed.has(d.id));
  if (others.length > 0) groups.push({ id: 'otros', title: 'Platos que no están en ninguna carta activa', dishes: others });
  return groups;
}

const UNITS_ES = { perKg: '/kg', per100g: '/100 g', market: 'S/M' };

/** '9,00 € (Media) · 15,00 € (Ración)', 'S/M' o 'Sin precio'. */
export function pricesSummaryEs(prices: PanelDishPrice[]): string {
  if (prices.length === 0) return 'Sin precio';
  return prices
    .map((p) => {
      const text = formatDishPrice(p, 'es', UNITS_ES);
      return p.labels.es ? `${text} (${p.labels.es})` : text;
    })
    .join(' · ');
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Escribir `data.ts`**

Create `src/lib/menu/data.ts`:

```ts
import 'server-only';
import type { RestaurantStatus } from '@/lib/auth/guards';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';
import { parseMenuTree, type MenuTree } from './tree';

/** Todo el menú del restaurante (también lo desactivado), con la sesión del usuario (RLS). */
export async function getMenuTree(restaurantId: string): Promise<MenuTree> {
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc('get_menu_tree', { p_restaurant_id: restaurantId });
  if (error) throw new Error(`get_menu_tree: ${error.message}`);
  return parseMenuTree(data);
}

export type QrBranding = {
  name: string;
  slug: string;
  status: RestaurantStatus;
  accentColor: string;
  logoPath: string | null;
};

/** Lo que lleva la tarjeta QR: nombre, logo y color de acento. */
export async function getQrBranding(restaurantId: string): Promise<QrBranding> {
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from('restaurants')
    .select('name, slug, status, accent_color, logo_path')
    .eq('id', restaurantId)
    .single();
  if (error || !data) throw new Error(`restaurants: ${error?.message ?? 'sin datos'}`);
  const r = data as { name: string; slug: string; status: RestaurantStatus; accent_color: string; logo_path: string | null };
  return { name: r.name, slug: r.slug, status: r.status, accentColor: r.accent_color, logoPath: r.logo_path };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Acciones de cartas, secciones, elementos y orden**

Create `src/lib/actions/menu.ts`:

```ts
'use server';

import { redirect } from 'next/navigation';
import { mapDbError } from '@/lib/db-errors';
import { fieldErrorsFrom, formStrings, type FormState } from '@/lib/forms';
import {
  confirmSchema,
  inlineItemSchema,
  menuFormSchema,
  readInlineItemForm,
  readMenuForm,
  readSectionForm,
  sectionFormSchema,
  unavailableModeSchema,
  type MenuFormValues,
} from '@/lib/menu/validation';
import { uuidSchema } from '@/lib/validation/schemas';
import { runPanelAction, throwIfError, type SupabaseServerClient } from './run';

type Table = 'menus' | 'menu_sections' | 'menu_items';

const isUuid = (value: unknown): value is string => typeof value === 'string' && uuidSchema.safeParse(value).success;

function field(fd: FormData, key: string): string {
  const value = fd.get(key);
  return typeof value === 'string' ? value : '';
}

/** Siguiente posición libre (al final) de una lista. */
async function nextPosition(supabase: SupabaseServerClient, table: Table, column: string, value: string): Promise<number> {
  const { data } = await supabase.from(table).select('position').eq(column, value).order('position', { ascending: false }).limit(1).maybeSingle();
  return ((data as { position: number } | null)?.position ?? -1) + 1;
}

/** UPDATE de una fila del restaurante con la sesión del usuario; 0 filas → sin permiso. */
async function updateOne(
  supabase: SupabaseServerClient,
  table: Table,
  id: string,
  restaurantId: string,
  values: Record<string, unknown>,
  success: string,
): Promise<FormState> {
  const { data, error } = await supabase.from(table).update(values).eq('id', id).eq('restaurant_id', restaurantId).select('id');
  if (error) return { error: mapDbError(error.message) };
  if (!data?.length) return { error: mapDbError('FORBIDDEN') };
  return { success };
}

async function deleteOne(supabase: SupabaseServerClient, table: Table, id: string, restaurantId: string, success: string): Promise<FormState> {
  const { data, error } = await supabase.from(table).delete().eq('id', id).eq('restaurant_id', restaurantId).select('id');
  if (error) return { error: mapDbError(error.message) };
  if (!data?.length) return { error: mapDbError('FORBIDDEN') };
  return { success };
}

/** Columnas de la carta (el tipo no cambia después de crearla). */
function menuColumns(v: MenuFormValues) {
  return {
    names: v.names,
    active: v.active,
    price_cents: v.priceCents,
    includes: v.includes,
    weekdays: v.weekdays,
    start_minute: v.startMinute,
    end_minute: v.endMinute,
    date_from: v.dateFrom,
    date_to: v.dateTo,
  };
}

// Cartas ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
export async function createMenu(_prev: FormState, fd: FormData): Promise<FormState> {
  const parsed = menuFormSchema.safeParse(readMenuForm(fd));
  if (!parsed.success) return { fieldErrors: fieldErrorsFrom(parsed.error) };
  const restaurantId = field(fd, 'restaurantId');
  const created = { id: '' };
  const result = await runPanelAction(restaurantId, 'menu.edit', async (supabase) => {
    const position = await nextPosition(supabase, 'menus', 'restaurant_id', restaurantId);
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('menus')
      .insert({ restaurant_id: restaurantId, kind: parsed.data.kind, position, ...menuColumns(parsed.data) })
      .select('id')
      .single();
    if (error) return { error: mapDbError(error.message) };
    created.id = (data as { id: string }).id;
    return { success: 'Carta creada.' };
  });
  if (created.id) redirect(`/panel/${restaurantId}/menu/${created.id}`);
  return result;
}

export async function updateMenu(_prev: FormState, fd: FormData): Promise<FormState> {
  const menuId = field(fd, 'menuId');
  if (!isUuid(menuId)) return { error: mapDbError(undefined) };
  const parsed = menuFormSchema.safeParse(readMenuForm(fd));
  if (!parsed.success) return { fieldErrors: fieldErrorsFrom(parsed.error) };
  const restaurantId = field(fd, 'restaurantId');
  return runPanelAction(restaurantId, 'menu.edit', (supabase) =>
    updateOne(supabase, 'menus', menuId, restaurantId, menuColumns(parsed.data), 'Carta guardada.'),
  );
}

/** Botón «Mostrar en la web» / «Ocultar de la web» de la lista de cartas. */
export async function setMenuActive(fd: FormData): Promise<void> {
  const menuId = field(fd, 'menuId');
  if (!isUuid(menuId)) return;
  const restaurantId = field(fd, 'restaurantId');
  const active = field(fd, 'active') === '1';
  const result = await runPanelAction(restaurantId, 'menu.edit', (supabase) =>
    updateOne(supabase, 'menus', menuId, restaurantId, { active }, active ? 'Carta visible.' : 'Carta oculta.'),
  );
  throwIfError(result);
}

export async function deleteMenu(_prev: FormState, fd: FormData): Promise<FormState> {
  const confirm = confirmSchema.safeParse(formStrings(fd, ['confirm']));
  if (!confirm.success) return { fieldErrors: fieldErrorsFrom(confirm.error) };
  const menuId = field(fd, 'menuId');
  if (!isUuid(menuId)) return { error: mapDbError(undefined) };
  const restaurantId = field(fd, 'restaurantId');
  const result = await runPanelAction(restaurantId, 'menu.edit', (supabase) =>
    deleteOne(supabase, 'menus', menuId, restaurantId, 'Carta borrada.'),
  );
  if (!result.error) redirect(`/panel/${restaurantId}/menu`);
  return result;
}

// Secciones -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
export async function addSection(_prev: FormState, fd: FormData): Promise<FormState> {
  const menuId = field(fd, 'menuId');
  if (!isUuid(menuId)) return { error: mapDbError(undefined) };
  const parsed = sectionFormSchema.safeParse(readSectionForm(fd));
  if (!parsed.success) return { fieldErrors: fieldErrorsFrom(parsed.error) };
  const restaurantId = field(fd, 'restaurantId');
  return runPanelAction(restaurantId, 'menu.edit', async (supabase) => {
    const position = await nextPosition(supabase, 'menu_sections', 'menu_id', menuId);
    const { error } = await supabase
      .from('menu_sections')
      .insert({ restaurant_id: restaurantId, menu_id: menuId, names: parsed.data.names, position });
    return error ? { error: mapDbError(error.message) } : { success: 'Sección añadida.' };
  });
}

export async function renameSection(_prev: FormState, fd: FormData): Promise<FormState> {
  const sectionId = field(fd, 'sectionId');
  if (!isUuid(sectionId)) return { error: mapDbError(undefined) };
  const parsed = sectionFormSchema.safeParse(readSectionForm(fd));
  if (!parsed.success) return { fieldErrors: fieldErrorsFrom(parsed.error) };
  const restaurantId = field(fd, 'restaurantId');
  return runPanelAction(restaurantId, 'menu.edit', (supabase) =>
    updateOne(supabase, 'menu_sections', sectionId, restaurantId, { names: parsed.data.names }, 'Sección guardada.'),
  );
}

export async function deleteSection(_prev: FormState, fd: FormData): Promise<FormState> {
  const confirm = confirmSchema.safeParse(formStrings(fd, ['confirm']));
  if (!confirm.success) return { fieldErrors: fieldErrorsFrom(confirm.error) };
  const sectionId = field(fd, 'sectionId');
  if (!isUuid(sectionId)) return { error: mapDbError(undefined) };
  const restaurantId = field(fd, 'restaurantId');
  return runPanelAction(restaurantId, 'menu.edit', (supabase) =>
    deleteOne(supabase, 'menu_sections', sectionId, restaurantId, 'Sección borrada. Los platos siguen en tu lista de platos.'),
  );
}

// Elementos de una sección ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
export async function addExistingDish(_prev: FormState, fd: FormData): Promise<FormState> {
  const sectionId = field(fd, 'sectionId');
  if (!isUuid(sectionId)) return { error: mapDbError(undefined) };
  const dishId = field(fd, 'dishId');
  if (!isUuid(dishId)) return { fieldErrors: { dishId: 'Elige un plato.' } };
  const restaurantId = field(fd, 'restaurantId');
  return runPanelAction(restaurantId, 'menu.edit', async (supabase) => {
    const position = await nextPosition(supabase, 'menu_items', 'section_id', sectionId);
    const { error } = await supabase
      .from('menu_items')
      .insert({ restaurant_id: restaurantId, section_id: sectionId, dish_id: dishId, position });
    return error ? { error: mapDbError(error.message) } : { success: 'Plato añadido.' };
  });
}

export async function addInlineItem(_prev: FormState, fd: FormData): Promise<FormState> {
  const sectionId = field(fd, 'sectionId');
  if (!isUuid(sectionId)) return { error: mapDbError(undefined) };
  const parsed = inlineItemSchema.safeParse(readInlineItemForm(fd));
  if (!parsed.success) return { fieldErrors: fieldErrorsFrom(parsed.error) };
  const restaurantId = field(fd, 'restaurantId');
  return runPanelAction(restaurantId, 'menu.edit', async (supabase) => {
    const position = await nextPosition(supabase, 'menu_items', 'section_id', sectionId);
    const { error } = await supabase.from('menu_items').insert({
      restaurant_id: restaurantId,
      section_id: sectionId,
      inline_names: parsed.data.names,
      inline_allergens: parsed.data.allergens,
      position,
    });
    return error ? { error: mapDbError(error.message) } : { success: 'Plato añadido a este menú.' };
  });
}

export async function updateInlineItem(_prev: FormState, fd: FormData): Promise<FormState> {
  const itemId = field(fd, 'itemId');
  if (!isUuid(itemId)) return { error: mapDbError(undefined) };
  const parsed = inlineItemSchema.safeParse(readInlineItemForm(fd));
  if (!parsed.success) return { fieldErrors: fieldErrorsFrom(parsed.error) };
  const restaurantId = field(fd, 'restaurantId');
  return runPanelAction(restaurantId, 'menu.edit', (supabase) =>
    updateOne(
      supabase,
      'menu_items',
      itemId,
      restaurantId,
      { inline_names: parsed.data.names, inline_allergens: parsed.data.allergens },
      'Plato guardado.',
    ),

}

/** Quita el plato de la sección (el plato sigue en la lista de platos). */
export async function removeItem(fd: FormData): Promise<void> {
  const itemId = field(fd, 'itemId');
  if (!isUuid(itemId)) return;
  const restaurantId = field(fd, 'restaurantId');
  const result = await runPanelAction(restaurantId, 'menu.edit', (supabase) =>
    deleteOne(supabase, 'menu_items', itemId, restaurantId, 'Quitado de la sección.'),
  );
  throwIfError(result);
}

// Orden (arrastrar o Subir/Bajar) -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
export type ReorderEntity = 'menus' | 'sections' | 'items';

const REORDER = {
  menus: { fn: 'reorder_menus', param: 'p_restaurant_id' },
  sections: { fn: 'reorder_sections', param: 'p_menu_id' },
  items: { fn: 'reorder_items', param: 'p_section_id' },
} as const;

/** Guarda el orden completo de una lista. parentId: el restaurante, la carta o la sección. */
export async function reorderMenuEntries(
  restaurantId: string,
  entity: ReorderEntity,
  parentId: string,
  ids: string[],
): Promise<FormState> {
  if (!(entity in REORDER) || !isUuid(parentId) || !Array.isArray(ids) || !ids.every(isUuid)) {
    return { error: mapDbError('INVALID_INPUT') };
  }
  const { fn, param } = REORDER[entity];
  return runPanelAction(restaurantId, 'menu.edit', async (supabase) => {
    const { error } = await supabase.rpc(fn, { [param]: parentId, p_ids: ids });
    return error ? { error: mapDbError(error.message) } : { success: 'Orden guardado.' };
  });
}

// Ajustes ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
export async function saveUnavailableMode(_prev: FormState, fd: FormData): Promise<FormState> {
  const parsed = unavailableModeSchema.safeParse(field(fd, 'mode'));
  if (!parsed.success) return { fieldErrors: { mode: 'Elige cómo se ven los platos agotados.' } };
  const restaurantId = field(fd, 'restaurantId');
  return runPanelAction(restaurantId, 'menu.edit', async (supabase) => {
    const { error } = await supabase.rpc('set_menu_unavailable_mode', { p_restaurant_id: restaurantId, p_mode: parsed.data });
    return error ? { error: mapDbError(error.message) } : { success: 'Guardado.' };
  });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Acciones de platos, foto y agotados**

Create `src/lib/actions/dishes.ts`:

```ts
'use server';

import { redirect } from 'next/navigation';
import { mapDbError } from '@/lib/db-errors';
import { fieldErrorsFrom, formStrings, type FormState } from '@/lib/forms';
import { checkUpload, dishPhotoPath, extensionFor, MEDIA_BUCKET } from '@/lib/media';
import { confirmSchema, dishFormSchema, readDishForm } from '@/lib/menu/validation';
import { uuidSchema } from '@/lib/validation/schemas';
import { runPanelAction, throwIfError, type SupabaseServerClient } from './run';

const isUuid = (value: unknown): value is string => typeof value === 'string' && uuidSchema.safeParse(value).success;

function field(fd: FormData, key: string): string {
  const value = fd.get(key);
  return typeof value === 'string' ? value : '';
}

function dishPage(restaurantId: string, dishId: string, query: Record<string, string>): string {
  const qs = new URLSearchParams(query).toString();
  return `/panel/${restaurantId}/menu/platos/${dishId}${qs ? `?${qs}` : ''}`;
}

async function currentPhoto(supabase: SupabaseServerClient, restaurantId: string, dishId: string): Promise<string | null> {
  const { data } = await supabase.from('dishes').select('photo_path').eq('id', dishId).eq('restaurant_id', restaurantId).maybeSingle();
  return (data as { photo_path: string | null } | null)?.photo_path ?? null;
}

/** Crea (sin dishId) o guarda un plato con todos sus precios de una vez (save_dish). */
export async function saveDish(_prev: FormState, fd: FormData): Promise<FormState> {
  const parsed = dishFormSchema.safeParse(readDishForm(fd));
  if (!parsed.success) return { fieldErrors: fieldErrorsFrom(parsed.error) };
  const restaurantId = field(fd, 'restaurantId');
  const dishId = field(fd, 'dishId');
  if (dishId && !isUuid(dishId)) return { error: mapDbError(undefined) };
  const sectionId = field(fd, 'sectionId');
  const menuId = field(fd, 'menuId');
  const d = parsed.data;
  const created = { id: '', warning: '' };
  const result = await runPanelAction(restaurantId, 'menu.edit', async (supabase) => {
    const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc('save_dish', {
      p_restaurant_id: restaurantId,
      p_dish_id: dishId || null,
      p_names: d.names,
      p_descriptions: d.descriptions,
      p_allergens: d.allergens,
      p_tags: d.tags,
      p_prices: d.prices.map((p) => ({ kind: p.kind, price_cents: p.priceCents, labels: p.labels })),
    });
    if (error) return { error: mapDbError(error.message) };
    if (dishId) return { success: 'Plato guardado.' };
    created.id = data as string;
    if (isUuid(sectionId)) {
      const { data: last } = await supabase
        .from('menu_items')
        .select('position')
        .eq('section_id', sectionId)
        .order('position', { ascending: false })
        .limit(1)
        .maybeSingle();
      const position = ((last as { position: number } | null)?.position ?? -1) + 1;
      const { error: itemError } = await supabase
        .from('menu_items')
        .insert({ restaurant_id: restaurantId, section_id: sectionId, dish_id: created.id, position });
      if (itemError) created.warning = 'no-seccion';
    }
    return { success: 'Plato creado.' };
  });
  if (created.id) {
    redirect(dishPage(restaurantId, created.id, { ...(isUuid(menuId) && { carta: menuId }), ...(created.warning && { aviso: created.warning }) }));
  }
  return result;
}

/** Duplica un plato. Desde una sección, la copia se queda al final de esa sección; si no, se abre la copia. */
export async function duplicateDish(fd: FormData): Promise<void> {
  const restaurantId = field(fd, 'restaurantId');
  const dishId = field(fd, 'dishId');
  if (!isUuid(dishId)) return;
  const sectionId = field(fd, 'sectionId');
  const menuId = field(fd, 'menuId');
  const created = { id: '' };
  const result = await runPanelAction(restaurantId, 'menu.edit', async (supabase) => {
    const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc('duplicate_dish', { p_dish_id: dishId, p_section_id: isUuid(sectionId) ? sectionId : null });
    if (error) return { error: mapDbError(error.message) };
    created.id = data as string;
    return { success: 'Plato duplicado.' };
  });
  throwIfError(result);
  if (!isUuid(sectionId)) redirect(dishPage(restaurantId, created.id, isUuid(menuId) ? { carta: menuId } : {}));
}

/** Borra el plato de todas las cartas y su foto. */
export async function deleteDish(_prev: FormState, fd: FormData): Promise<FormState> {
  const confirm = confirmSchema.safeParse(formStrings(fd, ['confirm']));
  if (!confirm.success) return { fieldErrors: fieldErrorsFrom(confirm.error) };
  const restaurantId = field(fd, 'restaurantId');
  const dishId = field(fd, 'dishId');
  if (!isUuid(dishId)) return { error: mapDbError(undefined) };
  const menuId = field(fd, 'menuId');
  const result = await runPanelAction(restaurantId, 'menu.edit', async (supabase) => {
    const { data, error } = await supabase.from('dishes').delete().eq('id', dishId).eq('restaurant_id', restaurantId).select('photo_path');
    if (error) return { error: mapDbError(error.message) };
    const row = (data as { photo_path: string | null }[] | null)?.[0];
    if (!row) return { error: mapDbError('FORBIDDEN') };
    if (row.photo_path) {
      const { error: removeError } = await supabase.storage.from(MEDIA_BUCKET).remove([row.photo_path]);
      if (removeError) console.error('[menu] no se pudo borrar la foto del plato', removeError.message);
    }
    return { success: 'Plato borrado.' };
  });
  if (!result.error) redirect(isUuid(menuId) ? `/panel/${restaurantId}/menu/${menuId}` : `/panel/${restaurantId}/menu/platos`);
  return result;
}

/** Sube la foto ya reducida en el navegador y sustituye la anterior. */
export async function uploadDishPhoto(fd: FormData): Promise<FormState> {
  const restaurantId = field(fd, 'restaurantId');
  const dishId = field(fd, 'dishId');
  const file = fd.get('file');
  if (!isUuid(dishId) || !(file instanceof File)) return { error: mapDbError(undefined) };
  const problem = checkUpload(file);
  if (problem) return { error: problem };
  const ext = extensionFor(file.type) ?? 'webp';
  return runPanelAction(restaurantId, 'menu.edit', async (supabase) => {
    const bucket = supabase.storage.from(MEDIA_BUCKET);
    const path = dishPhotoPath(restaurantId, ext, crypto.randomUUID());
    const { error: uploadError } = await bucket.upload(path, file, { contentType: file.type, cacheControl: '31536000', upsert: false });
    if (uploadError) return { error: mapDbError(uploadError.message) };
    const previous = await currentPhoto(supabase, restaurantId, dishId);
    const { data, error } = await supabase.from('dishes').update({ photo_path: path }).eq('id', dishId).eq('restaurant_id', restaurantId).select('id');
    if (error || !data?.length) {
      await bucket.remove([path]);
      return { error: mapDbError(error?.message ?? 'FORBIDDEN') };
    }
    if (previous) await bucket.remove([previous]);
    return { success: 'Foto actualizada.' };
  });
}

export async function removeDishPhoto(fd: FormData): Promise<void> {
  const restaurantId = field(fd, 'restaurantId');
  const dishId = field(fd, 'dishId');
  if (!isUuid(dishId)) return;
  const result = await runPanelAction(restaurantId, 'menu.edit', async (supabase) => {
    const previous = await currentPhoto(supabase, restaurantId, dishId);
    const { data, error } = await supabase.from('dishes').update({ photo_path: null }).eq('id', dishId).eq('restaurant_id', restaurantId).select('id');
    if (error || !data?.length) return { error: mapDbError(error?.message ?? 'FORBIDDEN') };
    if (previous) await supabase.storage.from(MEDIA_BUCKET).remove([previous]);
    return { success: 'Foto quitada.' };
  });
  throwIfError(result);
}

/** Interruptor de agotado (también Sala): solo por la función set_dish_unavailable. */
export async function setDishUnavailable(restaurantId: string, dishId: string, unavailable: boolean): Promise<FormState> {
  if (!isUuid(dishId) || typeof unavailable !== 'boolean') return { error: mapDbError('INVALID_INPUT') };
  return runPanelAction(restaurantId, 'menu.toggle_unavailable', async (supabase) => {
    const { error } = await supabase.rpc('set_dish_unavailable', { p_dish_id: dishId, p_unavailable: unavailable });
    if (error) return { error: mapDbError(error.message) };
    return { success: unavailable ? 'Marcado como agotado.' : 'Vuelve a estar disponible.' };
  });
}
```
